Yet in niche domains, where data is scarce, high-dimensional, and shifted from the pretraining distribution, they may still fail to outperform carefully designed domain-specific methods. Many such domains also provide curated relational knowledge in the form of knowledge graphs and knowledge banks, but how to use this knowledge to improve and steer \textit{small} specialist tabular foundation models remains unclear. We address this problem through \textbf{Know}ledge-informed fine-tuning of \textbf{s}mall \textbf{T}abular \textbf{F}oundation \textbf{M}odels (\modelname). Specifically, we study nanoscale TabPFN- and TabICL-style variants, pretrained under controlled synthetic prior families and adapted using two complementary mechanisms: structural attention priors derived from knowledge graphs and parameter-efficient low-rank updates. We show that injecting domain-specific structural knowledge during fine-tuning yields meaningful gains over vanilla variants in specialist settings, whereas gains on general-domain tasks are marginal. We further observe that continual fine-tuning of frontier models can trigger collapse of pretrained knowledge and mechanisms.

</details>


### [178] [When Is a Draft Accepted? A Theory of Acceptance in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30265)
*Aaryam Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为推测解码中的贪婪解码、松弛接受准则和树状候选集等实际应用场景建立了理论框架，通过表征拒绝区域的KL散度，推导出严格的认证证书和边界界限，并在Qwen3模型上验证了松弛和树状准则能显著扩大认证接受区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码理论主要研究保持分布的随机设置，但在实际系统中，贪婪解码、松弛接受规则和树状候选集被广泛使用，这些场景的成功由局部排序和阈值事件决定，而非精确的分布等价性，因此缺乏相应的理论支撑。

Method: 识别常见接受准则的拒绝区域可表征为目标分布的下水平集；对这些区域，刻画导致拒绝所需的精确KL散度，从而获得严格认证证书和基于边界的锐利界限；将框架扩展到贪婪树状解码，推导目标贪婪词元是否被草稿模型top-(m)候选覆盖的精确认证。

Result: 为严格贪婪解码、加性和乘性松弛接受、top-(m)松弛准则以及熵阈值接受等方法提供了精确认证证书和边界界限；在Qwen3模型上的实验表明，松弛和树状准则显著扩大了认证接受区域，尤其在目标模型分布边界较低的解码步骤上效果明显。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了推测解码在实际确定性局部接受事件方面的理论空白，为实践中常用的非分布保持推测解码方法提供了严格的理论分析和可验证的接受保证，补充了现有的分布保持分析方法。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language model inference by using a fast drafter to propose candidate tokens that are then verified by a larger target model. Existing theory largely studies the stochastic, distribution-preserving setting, where the goal is to exactly sample from the target distribution. In contrast, many practical systems use greedy decoding, relaxed acceptance rules, or tree-based candidate sets, where success is governed by local ranking and threshold events rather than exact distributional equality. We develop a theory for these regimes. We identify that many common acceptance criteria have rejection regions that can be characterized as lower level sets of the target distribution. For these, we characterize the exact KL divergence required for rejection yielding exact certificates and sharp margin-based bounds for strict greedy decoding, additive and multiplicative relaxed acceptance, top-(m) relaxed criteria, and entropy-thresholded acceptance. We then extend the framework to greedy tree decoding, deriving exact and margin-only certificates for when the target greedy token remains covered by the drafter's top-(m) candidates. Finally, we evaluate the resulting certificates on Qwen3 models, showing that relaxed and tree-based criteria substantially enlarge the region of certified acceptance, especially on decoding steps with low target model distribution margin. These results complement existing distribution-preserving analyses of speculative decoding by characterizing the deterministic local acceptance events common in practical inference systems.

</details>


### [179] [Towards Continual Motion-Language Agents: LoRA Variants for Incremental Motion Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30266)
*Bertram Taetz,Hugo Albuquerque Cosme da Silva,Gabriele Bleser-Taetz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究运动-语言智能体在持续学习场景下的双向能力（运动生成与理解），通过引入基于自动编码器路由器的混合专家架构，在无需任务标签的情况下实现了近乎零遗忘的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型在静态设置下表现优异，但在动态环境中运行的自主智能体需要持续学习新的运动概念（如新颖的运动风格或特定手势），同时避免对已习得技能的灾难性遗忘。运动-语言双向学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡问题亟待解决。

Method: 基于冻结的大型语言模型骨干网络，引入低秩适应（LoRA）变体以缓解任务间干扰。提出混合专家架构，利用基于自动编码器的路由器在推理时选择任务特定的专家，无需任务标签。基于HumanML3D数据集，通过运动描述的语义聚类建立可复现的五任务基准进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，在运动生成（T2M）和运动理解（M2T）两个方向上均实现了近乎零遗忘，同时保持高质量的生成和描述能力。硬专家选择（通过路由）在质量指标上显著优于软专家混合，表明在持续学习设置中保持专家隔离对维持性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 专家隔离对运动-语言智能体的持续学习性能维护至关重要。发现token级别准确率与下游生成质量之间可能出现分歧，突显了未来终身运动-语言智能体研究需要更全面的评估协议。

Abstract: Motion-language agents must possess the bidirectional capability to both understand human movement (motion-to-text, M2T) and generate it from natural language (text-to-motion, T2M). While foundational models have achieved strong performance in static settings, autonomous agents operating in dynamic environments must continuously incorporate new motion concepts -- such as novel athletic styles or specialized gestures -- without catastrophic forgetting of previously acquired skills. We investigate the stability-plasticity trade-off in bidirectional motion-language learning under sequential task exposure. Building on a frozen large language model backbone, we introduce low-rank adaptation (LoRA) variants designed to mitigate inter-task interference. We specifically propose mixture-of-experts architectures that utilize an autoencoder-based router to select task-specific experts at inference time, so that no task-label is needed. To evaluate these methods, we establish a reproducible five-task benchmark derived from HumanML3D through semantic clustering of motion descriptions. Our experimental results demonstrate near-zero forgetting across both M2T and T2M directions while maintaining high generation and captioning quality. Furthermore, we show that hard expert selection via routing significantly outperforms soft expert blending in quality metrics, indicating that preserving expert isolation is critical for maintaining performance in our continual learning setting. Finally, we observe that a divergence between token-level accuracy and downstream generation quality may occur, highlighting the need for more comprehensive evaluation protocols in future research on lifelong motion-language agents.

</details>


### [180] [DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview: A Low-Compute Real-Time Controllable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30292)
*Daniyel Ayupov,Artur Markov-Tsoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview，一个面向实时交互式世界模拟的预览基础世界模型。该系统基于LongLive 1自回归视频架构（源自Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B）并融入了受Matrix-Game系列启发的残差动作路径。其核心优势在于低计算量适配、消费级GPU运行和广泛的交互能力覆盖，支持实时键盘鼠标控制、多模态初始化、中流重提示、双视角操作以及原生480p分辨率下的分钟级交互推演，在单张RTX 4090上可达14-15 FPS且内存占用低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿规模的世界模拟器通常需要大量计算资源，难以在消费级硬件上实时运行。本文旨在探索一条互补的技术路径：通过低成本适配实现可在消费级GPU上实时运行、具备广泛交互能力的可控世界模型预览系统。

Method: 该方法基于开源视频骨干网络Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B，通过LongLive 1自回归视频架构进行扩展，并引入受Matrix-Game系列启发的残差动作路径以实现交互控制。系统采用针对性的适配训练策略，在保持高成本效益的同时实现多种交互功能，包括实时键鼠控制、多模态初始化、中流重提示和双视角操作等。

Result: DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview成功实现了在单张RTX 4090消费级GPU上的实时运行，达到14-15 FPS的帧率，内存占用低。系统能够以原生480p分辨率进行分钟级长度的交互式推演，支持多种交互模式，证明了低成本适配方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 尽管DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview在记忆完整性和前沿质量方面仍有不足，但成功展示了一条实用的低计算量路径，能够在消费级GPU上实现实时可控的世界模型预览，为未来的世界模拟器发展提供了重要的技术参考。

Abstract: We present DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview, a preview foundational world model for real-time interactive world simulation. The system adapts the LongLive 1 autoregressive video stack, itself derived from Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, with a residual action pathway inspired by the Matrix-Game family. DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview focuses on a complementary axis to frontier-scale world simulators: low-compute adaptation, consumer-GPU runtime, and broad interactive capability coverage. It supports live keyboard and mouse control, multimodal initialization, mid-stream reprompting, dual-view operation, and minute-scale interactive rollouts at native 480p resolution, reaching up to 14 to 15 FPS FPS on a single RTX 4090 with a low memory footprint. By leveraging open video backbones and applying targeted adaptation runs, we build the preview system with high cost-efficiency. DF-World 0.1 Preview is not yet a memory-complete or frontier-quality world simulator, but demonstrates a practical low-compute route toward real-time controllable world-model previews on consumer GPUs.

</details>


### [181] [Toward an Energy-Optimized Operation of Data Centers Located in Wind Farms Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30316)
*Jan Stenner,Alexander Kilian,Sebastian Peitz,Hermann de Meer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习用于风电集成HPC数据中心的负载调度，发现纯RL存在信用分配问题，通过模仿学习和奖励塑形改进性能，但仍与离线优化器存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强化学习作为在线控制器在风电集成高性能计算数据中心中实现缩减感知的工作负载转移问题，旨在利用波动性风电能源优化数据中心能耗管理。

Method: 提出可复现的固定日仿真框架（包含合成风力和价格信号、延迟完成反馈）；在单风电-单数据中心场景下，采用PPO和改进的SAC算法，结合模仿学习（基于优化）和基于势能的奖励塑形两种对策来解决信用分配问题。

Result: 纯强化学习存在明显信用分配问题，倾向于在一天早期低效利用免费风能；模仿学习和奖励塑形在相关配置下均提供改进；PPO和改进版SAC表现最佳；但与离线优化器仍存在性能差距（因RL在线决策无未来信息）。

Conclusion: 构建了透明的基准和消融实验基础，为将方法扩展到更丰富的多站点和连续时间场景提供了可扩展的研究框架。

Abstract: This paper studies Reinforcement Learning as an online controller for curtailment-aware workload shifting in wind-turbine-integrated high-performance computing (HPC) data centers. We introduce a reproducible fixed-day simulation framework with synthetic wind and price signals and delayed completion feedback, designed to be extensible toward more complex scenarios. As a controlled benchmarking basis, we then focus on the minimal case with one wind turbine and one co-located data center. In this setting, pure Reinforcement Learning exhibits a pronounced credit-assignment problem and tends to underuse free wind energy early in the day. We therefore evaluate two complementary countermeasures: optimization-based Imitation Learning and potential-based Reward Shaping. Across multi-seed training and a 200-day test set, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) variant with an additional on-policy update routine achieve strong empirical performance among learned policies, and both Imitation Learning and Reward Shaping provide improvements in relevant configurations. A performance gap to the optimizer remains, which is expected: the optimizer plans offline with full-day foresight, whereas Reinforcement Learning must decide online from current observations without future realizations. The benchmark and ablation results provide a transparent basis for extending the approach toward richer multi-site and continuous-time scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [Hybrid Active-Online Learning Framework for Label-Efficient Concept Drift Adaptation in Optical Network Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30322)
*Yousuf Moiz Ali,Jaroslaw E. Prilepsky,João Pedro,Sasipim Srivallapanondh,Antonio Napoli,Sergei K. Turitsyn,Pedro Freire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合主动-在线学习框架用于光网络故障检测，仅需查询3.4%的流样本即可达到接近上限的准确率和AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光网络故障检测中存在概念漂移问题，传统方法需要大量标注样本，成本高昂且难以适应动态变化的网络环境。

Method: 提出混合主动-在线学习框架，采用基于边界的选择性标注策略，仅对关键样本进行标注查询，以适应概念漂移。

Result: 在仅查询3.4%流样本的情况下，准确率和AUC分数接近上限性能，相比静态推理的延迟开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了标签高效的概念漂移自适应，在保持高检测性能的同时大幅降低标注成本和系统延迟。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid active-online learning framework for label-efficient concept drift adaptation in optical network failure detection. Using margin-based selective labeling, our method achieves nearceiling accuracy and AUC scores while querying only 3.4% of streaming samples, with negligible latency overhead compared to static inference.

</details>


### [183] [FlexTab: A Flexible Encoder-Decoder Architecture for In-Context Learning Across Diverse Tabular Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30336)
*Marek Polewczyk,Maximilian Schambach,Marco Spinaci,Sam Thelin,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlexTab，一种灵活的表格数据上下文学习架构，通过任务无关编码器和任务特定解码器的组合，在多个表格预测任务上实现领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格上下文学习方法将特征表示与特定预测目标耦合，缺乏灵活性。需要一种能生成目标无关的行嵌入表示，并在多种下游任务中复用的通用架构。

Method: 设计一个任务无关的单一编码器，生成目标无关的行嵌入；配备多个任务特定的解码器，分别处理分类、回归、异常检测、聚类、实体匹配和关系数据库实体分类六种任务。编码器和解码器均在大规模真实无标注表格语料上训练。

Result: FlexTab在分类、回归、异常检测和实体匹配四个任务上达到SOTA性能；在关系数据库实体分类任务上与专用模型表现相当。验证了单一共享编码器配合任务特定解码器可作为多种表格预测问题的有效通用骨干。

Conclusion: 证明了任务无关编码器与任务特定解码器的解耦设计能够有效支持多样化的表格预测任务，为构建通用表格数据学习系统提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: We introduce FlexTab, a flexible encoder-decoder architecture for in-context learning on tabular data that pairs a single, task-agnostic encoder with a suite of task-specific decoders. Unlike existing tabular in-context learners, which entangle feature representations with a specific prediction target, our design produces \textit{target-agnostic} row embeddings that can be leveraged across a wide range of downstream tasks within a table-native in-context learning setup. We demonstrate this flexibility on six distinct problems: classification, regression, anomaly detection, clustering, entity matching, and entity classification in relational databases. Both the encoder and the task-specific decoders are trained on a large corpus of real-world, unlabeled tables. FlexTab achieves state-of-the-art performance on classification, regression, anomaly detection and entity matching, while remaining competitive with specialized models on entity classification in a relational setting. These results demonstrate that a single shared encoder, paired with task-specific decoders, can serve as an effective general-purpose backbone for diverse tabular prediction problems. The inference code and checkpoints will be made publicly available at https://github.com/SAP-samples/flextab.

</details>


### [184] [DRIFT: Difficulty Routing Self-DIstillation with Rhythm-Gated Exploration and Success BuFfer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30345)
*Haisen Luo,Yiwei Liu,Haoning Wang,Dan Liu,Junxi Yin,Haotian Wang,Lei Zhang,Xiaoyu Tian,Shuaiting Chen,Yuansheng Song,Baoyan Guo,Xiongfei Yan,Bolan Yang,Chengwei Liu,Ming Cui,Jiong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DRIFT框架，通过难度路由和节奏门控机制实现大语言模型的在线自我进化，在五个基准测试上平均得分79.5%，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏和强化学习方法缺乏显式机制来跟踪问题级别的学习进度并相应调整优化策略，导致训练可能过度优化简单问题、从困难问题获得弱监督，且未能充分探索边界案例。

Method: 提出DRIFT在线自我进化策略优化框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)难度路由：识别问题级别的学习状态，动态分配自蒸馏和强化学习信号；(2)节奏门控：在token级别细化策略更新，将探索集中在关键推理位置。同时引入成功缓冲区和两阶段课程学习策略，保留高质量历史经验并渐进引导模型从可靠行为获取转向稳定策略演化。

Result: 在五个基准测试和三个模型规模上验证，DRIFT在所有评估指标上均超越GRPO和SDPO的峰值性能。五个基准测试平均得分达79.5%，分别超越GRPO 9.5%和SDPO 7.5%；在ToolUse任务上准确率达79.2%，分别超越GRPO 13.5%和SDPO 10.7%，建立新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: DRIFT成功实现了大语言模型在无外部专家监督下的稳定自我改进，通过问题级别和token级别的双重调节机制，有效解决了现有方法中的优化不平衡问题，在多项任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Enabling large language models to achieve stable self-improvement without external expert supervision remains a central challenge in complex reasoning tasks. Existing self-distillation and reinforcement learning methods lack explicit mechanisms for tracking problem-level learning progress and adapting optimization strategies accordingly. Consequently, training may over-optimize easy problems, receive weak supervision from hard problems, and fail to sufficiently explore borderline cases. To resolve these issues, we propose DRIFT, an online self-evolution policy optimization framework for large language models. DRIFT regulates the model's self-improvement process through the joint use of Difficulty Routing and Rhythm Gating. The former identifies the model's learning state at the problem level and dynamically allocates self-distillation and reinforcement learning signals, while the latter refines policy updates at the token level, concentrating exploration on critical reasoning positions. By further incorporating a success buffer and a two-stage curriculum learning strategy, DRIFT preserves high-quality historical experience while progressively guiding the model from reliable behavior acquisition toward stable policy evolution. Evaluated across five benchmarks and three model scales, DRIFT surpasses the peak performance of both GRPO and SDPO across all evaluated metrics. On the average score over the five benchmarks, DRIFT achieves 79.5$\%$, outperforming GRPO by 9.5$\%$ and SDPO by 7.5$\%$, establishing a new state-of-the-art result. Notably, on ToolUse, DRIFT reaches an accuracy of 79.2$\%$, improving over GRPO by 13.5$\%$ and SDPO by 10.7$\%$, setting a new state-of-the-art and substantially outperforming all concurrent methods.

</details>


### [185] [On the Vulnerability of Parameter-Level Defenses to Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30360)
*Kuangpu Guo,Qingyan Zheng,Jian Liang,Yongcan Yu,Zilei Wang,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有基于参数变换的模型合并防御方法的脆弱性，提出了Anchor-Guided Attack (AGA)攻击方法成功绕过防御，并设计了Anchor-Repulsive Fine-tuning (ARF)防御方法进行应对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并技术允许攻击者在未经授权的情况下组合专家模型，现有防御方法采用线性参数变换来保护模型。然而，这些防御方法的安全性尚未得到系统性分析，存在潜在的安全漏洞需要深入研究。

Method: 发现受保护的任务向量幅值较小，导致受保护权重主要由预训练模型主导。以此为突破口，提出AGA攻击方法：将预训练模型作为静态参考锚点，通过将受保护模型与锚点对齐来解析地恢复变换矩阵。同时提出ARF防御方法，通过减少锚点主导性来抵御AGA攻击。

Result: 在现实的无防御知识场景下，AGA能够持续有效地绕过单个防御和组合防御。ARF防御方法经过实验验证，能够有效击败所提出的AGA攻击。

Conclusion: 现有基于参数变换的防御方法存在根本性缺陷，无法有效抵御精心设计的攻击。提出的AGA攻击揭示了这一脆弱性，而ARF防御为构建更鲁棒的模型保护机制提供了新思路。

Abstract: The training-free integration of expert models via model merging has exposed significant security risks, enabling free-riders to combine specialized models without authorization. Recent works propose parameter-level defenses that employ linear parameter transformations to neutralize this threat. In this paper, we systematically analyze such defenses and reveal that their protected task vectors are inherently small in magnitude. Consequently, the protected weights remain overwhelmingly dominated by the pretrained model. Based on this observation, we designate the pretrained model as a static reference anchor and propose the Anchor-Guided Attack (AGA) to circumvent existing safeguards. Specifically, AGA aligns the protected model with this anchor to recover the transformation matrix analytically. Extensive evaluations validate that AGA consistently bypasses both individual and composite defenses under realistic defense-agnostic scenarios. Furthermore, we provide Anchor-Repulsive Fine-tuning (ARF), a defense method to mitigate the anchor dominance leveraged by AGA. Empirical results confirm that ARF effectively defeats the proposed attack. Our code is available at https://github.com/krumpguo/secure-merge-attack.

</details>


### [186] [FlowAWR: Online Adaptive Flow Reinforcement via Advantage-Weighted Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30376)
*Zheming Fu,Ruizhe He,Wei Shang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Lei Wang,Chang Liu,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowAWR方法，通过将连续生成策略优化重构为向理论最优速度场的监督回归，实现了SDE-Free优化和CFG-Free生成，在SD3.5-Medium上对齐性能提升2-5倍收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续空间生成流模型的在线强化学习对齐方法受限于不可处理的轨迹似然。基于密度近似的策略梯度方法依赖随机SDE采样器构建可处理的转移核，导致训练-推理不一致并需要无分类器引导（CFG）；而隐式框架DiffusionNFT直接优化正向过程速度场时，其启发式固定幅度修正无法根据组内相对质量调整优化强度。

Method: 提出Flow Advantage-Weighted Rectification (FlowAWR)范式，从KL约束奖励最大化的最优策略出发，推导出最优速度场，该速度场具有幅度感知的优势加权修正形式，将连续生成策略优化重构为向理论最优速度场的监督回归任务，实现无SDE优化和无CFG生成。

Result: 在SD3.5-Medium上的对比实验中，FlowAWR实现了优于DiffusionNFT的对齐性能，同时收敛速度提升2-5倍（例如在1.2k步达到24.12 PickScore，而DiffusionNFT需2.0k步达到23.82，FlowGRPO需>4k步达到23.50）。在多奖励约束下，FlowAWR能够保持生成质量，满足结构规则并维持稳定的域外性能。

Conclusion: FlowAWR成功解决了现有生成流模型对齐方法中的训练-推理不一致问题和启发式修正的局限性，通过理论推导的最优速度场形式，实现了高效、稳定的连续空间生成模型对齐，为后续研究生成模型的强化学习对齐提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Aligning generative flow models on continuous spaces via online reinforcement learning is constrained by intractable trajectory likelihoods. Existing density-approximated policy gradient methods rely on stochastic SDE samplers to construct tractable transition kernels, which introduce training-inference inconsistencies and necessitates Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG).
  While implicit frameworks such as DiffusionNFT directly optimize forward-process velocity fields, its heuristic fixed-magnitude corrections prevent optimization strength from relative intra-group quality.
  We propose \textit{Flow Advantage-Weighted Rectification} (\textbf{FlowAWR}), a paradigm that recasts continuous generative policy optimization as supervised regression toward a theoretically optimal velocity field.
  Starting from the optimal policy of a KL-constrained reward maximization, FlowAWR derives the optimal velocity field that admits a magnitude-aware, advantage-weighted rectification form, yielding SDE-free optimization and CFG-free generation.
  In comparative evaluations on SD3.5-Medium, FlowAWR achieves improved alignment performance alongside a 2$\times$ to 5$\times$ convergence acceleration over DiffusionNFT (e.g., reaching a 24.12 PickScore in 1.2k steps, versus 23.82 in 2.0k steps for DiffusionNFT and 23.50 in $>$4k steps for FlowGRPO). Under multi-reward constraints, FlowAWR sustains generation quality, satisfying structural rules while maintaining stable out-of-domain performance.

</details>


### [187] [Scalar Representations of Neural Network Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30384)
*Pedro Jiménez-González,Miguel C. Soriano,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将神经网络训练轨迹视为时序网络并进行标量嵌入，成功在高维参数空间中提取低维表示，保留了动力学特征并定义了特征时间，为神经网络优化轨迹研究提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络训练可视为在高维损失景观中演化的轨迹，但大量可训练参数使得直接分析这些动力学具有挑战性。现有方法难以有效处理高维参数空间的动态特性，因此需要一种能够捕捉训练动力学本质特征的低维表示方法。

Method: 将神经网络训练轨迹视为时序网络（temporal networks），应用最近提出的时序网络标量嵌入策略。使用在MNIST分类任务上训练的多层感知机作为模型，通过该嵌入方法获得低维表示，并分析其是否保留原始参数空间的主要动力学特征。

Result: 标量嵌入成功保留了原始参数空间的主要动力学特征，包括：（1）特定学习率区间下对初始条件敏感性的涌现；（2）准确重建网络的最大李雅普诺夫指数。基于嵌入标量轨迹定义了特征时间（类似李雅普诺夫时间），捕捉了原始高维系统中初始接近网络轨迹间的典型去相关时间。渐近训练状态的统计组织分布经重新缩放后在不同初始条件下坍缩为共同形式，与偏斜对数正态分布兼容。

Conclusion: 标量低维嵌入为研究和可视化神经网络优化轨迹的动力学性质提供了有用框架，能够有效捕捉高维参数空间中的关键动力学特征，为理解神经网络训练动力学提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Training in artificial neural networks can be viewed as a trajectory evolving through a high-dimensional loss landscape. However, the large number of trainable parameters makes the direct analysis of these dynamics challenging. In this work, we treat such training trajectories as temporal networks and apply recently proposed strategies for the scalar embedding of temporal networks. We investigate whether such a scalar embedding provides a meaningful low-dimensional representation of neural network training dynamics. Using a multilayer perceptron trained on the MNIST classification task, we show that the embedding preserves the main dynamical features observed in the original parameter space, including the emergence of sensitivity to initial conditions for specific learning rate regimes and an accurate reconstruction of the network's maximum Lyapunov exponent. We then use the embedded scalar trajectory to define a characteristic time, analogous to a Lyapunov time, after which the exponential separation between initially close embedded trajectories saturates. This characteristic time captures the typical decorrelation time between initially close network trajectories in the original high-dimensional system. Finally, we investigate the statistical organization of asymptotic training states through a spacing observable defined in the embedded space. We find that the distributions of rescaled asymptotic spacings collapse onto a common form across initial conditions and are compatible with a skew lognormal distribution. Altogether, our results suggest that scalar low-dimensional embeddings provide a useful framework for studying and visualizing the dynamical properties of neural network optimization trajectories.

</details>


### [188] [Predict, Reuse, and Repair: Accelerating Dynamic Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30389)
*Tianyu Wang,Gourav Rattihalli,Aditya Dhakal,Junbo Li,Zhiwei Ren,Dejan Milojicic,Longfei Shangguan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRR通过推测-重用-修复机制，利用动态稀疏注意力选择的时间局部性来消除选择-注意力串行依赖，降低解码延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏注意力（DSA）通过只关注与每个查询最相关的Top-K KV块来加速长上下文LLM解码，但它引入了串行化的选择-注意力依赖，成为新的延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出PRR运行时框架：使用基于EMA的轻量预测器利用DSA选择的时间局部性预测可能的块，在选择过程进行时并行推测性地计算注意力，并在得知真实选择集合后通过FlashAttention修复未命中的块。采用分析引导的推测预算，将推测工作保持在关键路径之外，并利用在线softmax统计信息将遗漏块融入部分注意力状态。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试和代表性DSA方法上，PRR将每个token解码延迟降低了高达40%，同时保持了下游任务的准确性。

Conclusion: PRR成功消除了DSA中的选择-注意力串行依赖，为长上下文LLM解码提供了显著的性能改进，同时不损害模型质量。

Abstract: Dynamic sparse attention (DSA) accelerates long-context LLM decoding by attending to only the top-K KV blocks relevant to each query, but it introduces a serialized selection-to-attention dependency that emerges as a new latency bottleneck. We present PRR, a speculate-reuse-repair runtime that exploits temporal locality in DSA selections to predict likely blocks, speculate the attention over them while selection is in flight, and incrementally repair missed blocks once the true selected set is known. PRR uses a lightweight EMA-based predictor, a profiling-guided speculation budget that keeps speculative work off the critical path, and a FlashAttention-based repair kernel that folds missed blocks into the partial attention state using online-softmax statistics. Across long-context benchmarks and representative DSA methods, PRR reduces per-token decoding latency by up to 40% while preserving downstream task accuracy. Github: https://github.com/Tianyu9748/Incremental_FlashAttention

</details>


### [189] [Beyond IID: How General Are Tabular Foundation Models, Really?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30410)
*Lennart Purucker,Andrej Tschalzev,Nick Erickson,Gioia Blayer,David Holzmüller,Alan Arazi,Alexander Pfefferle,Mustafa Tajjar,Gaël Varoquaux,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BeyondArena基准和Data Foundry框架，通过142个数据集对11个模型进行统一评估，发现现有表格基础模型仅在中小型IID数据上表现优异，而传统树模型和深度学习在非IID、大规模和高维数据上仍占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格基础模型评估分散在特定任务和学科中，基准软件和评估协议碎片化，导致模型研究者主要依赖标准基准进行测试，而这些基准主要集中在模型已表现优异的任务上，忽略了最具挑战性的场景，限制了领域的实质性进展。

Method: 开发了BeyondArena统一基准测试平台，支持多样化任务类型（IID、时间序列、分组数据），覆盖不同样本规模和特征维度，包含文本和高基数特征等多种特征类型；同时引入Data Foundry Python框架和元数据模式，用于为预测性机器学习策划表格数据集。

Result: 在11个模型和142个策划数据集上的评估显示：现有表格基础模型在中小型IID数据上表现优异；传统树模型和深度学习模型在非IID数据、大规模数据集和高维数据上仍然占主导地位。

Conclusion: BeyondArena为表格数据中最具挑战性的问题提供了研究指导，推动了面向更具挑战性场景的模型研究，有助于实现真正具备基础能力的表格模型的发展。

Abstract: Foundation models for predictive machine learning on tabular data have recently gained significant traction in academia and industry. Research communities across disciplines are increasingly evaluating tabular foundation models on diverse datasets and tasks. However, these task- and discipline-specific evaluations remain largely inaccessible to model researchers because benchmark software and evaluation protocols are fragmented. As a result, model researchers rely on standard benchmarks, which are mostly defined for tasks where tabular foundation models already excel. The most challenging scenarios are excluded, limiting meaningful progress in the field by focusing on marginal improvements on IID data rather than on broader, more demanding challenges. To overcome this, we introduce BeyondArena, the first unified holistic benchmark for tabular data that supports diverse task types (IID, temporal, grouped), across sample size and feature dimensionality scales, with diverse feature types (with text, with high cardinality) from a broad range of disciplines. To enable unified benchmarking beyond standard benchmarks, we introduce Data Foundry, a Python framework and metadata schema for curating tabular datasets for predictive machine learning. Our results across 11 models and 142 curated datasets show that existing tabular foundation models excel on tiny- to medium-sized IID data, while traditional tree-based and deep learning models still dominate on non-IID, large, and high-dimensional datasets. BeyondArena guides model research for the most demanding challenges in tabular data, enabling progress towards truly foundational tabular models.

</details>


### [190] [Diffusion Fine-tuning with Rewarded Moment Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30414)
*Alexis Jacq,Guillaume Couairon,Valentin De Bortoli,Quentin Berthet,Arnaud Doucet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RMMD框架，将扩散模型蒸馏与强化学习微调相结合，在保持生成质量的同时优化奖励函数，在ImageNet和天气预报任务上均取得显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型后训练中的蒸馏和强化学习微调通常被独立研究，两者之间的交互机制，尤其是微调如何影响蒸馏模型的生成质量，目前尚不清楚。需要统一框架来同时实现模型压缩和奖励优化。

Method: 提出奖励矩匹配蒸馏(RMMD)框架，通过调整采样循环进行on-policy训练，并将蒸馏损失重新设计为积分KL正则化的代理，从而在最大化奖励函数的同时保持蒸馏模型的高保真度特性。

Result: 在ImageNet上，RMMD在FID-Reward帕累托前沿上优于单步基线(DI++)和多步竞争对手(DRaFT, HyperNoise)。应用于GenCast天气预报模型时，实现了7.5倍加速，在93%的目标天气变量上超越教师模型，且具有更好的校准性能。

Conclusion: RMMD成功统一了蒸馏和强化学习微调两个阶段，证明了该方法能够扩展到复杂的高维科学领域，为扩散模型后训练提供了有效的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Distillation and Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning are the primary pillars of diffusion post-training. While traditionally studied in isolation, the interaction between these phases remains poorly understood, and in particular how fine-tuning impacts the generative quality of distilled models. We introduce Rewarded Moment Matching Distillation (RMMD), a novel framework that simultaneously distills diffusion models and maximizes a reward function. RMMD preserves the high-fidelity ``naturalness'' characteristic of advanced distillation (such as 8-step Moment Matching) by adapting the sampling loop for on-policy training and repurposing the distillation loss as a proxy for integral KL regularization. By evaluating the FID-Reward Pareto fronts on ImageNet, we demonstrate that RMMD achieves superior trade-offs compared to single-step baselines (DI++) and multi-step competitors (DRaFT, HyperNoise). Finally, we apply RMMD to GenCast, a state-of-the-art weather forecasting model, to distill it while optimizing the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS) metric. The resulting distilled model achieves a 7.5x speedup while outperforming the teacher model on 93% of target weather variables, and being better calibrated. This proves that RMMD scales to complex, high-dimensional scientific domains.

</details>


### [191] [Experience Augmented Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30420)
*Jinda Lu,Kexin Huang,Junkang Wu,Shuo Yang,Jinghan Li,Chiyu Ma,Shaohang Wei,Xiang Wang,Guoyin Wang,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EAPO方法，通过策略自适应的方式重用经验，解决了RLVR中采样成本高和固定轨迹重用导致的策略失配问题，在多个基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）方法通常从头进行在线策略优化，导致采样成本高且经验利用效率低。虽然尝试重用固定推理轨迹，但随着训练过程中模型能力和策略行为的演变，这种固定轨迹重用方法存在策略失配问题。

Method: 提出经验增强策略优化（EAPO）方法，将先前的RL优化策略作为动作级经验先验，在rollout过程的关键决策点选择性地注入经验。为确保从经验增强的rollout中稳定且无偏地学习，EAPO还结合了自适应重要性采样方案。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5-math 7b和Qwen-3-8B模型上的实验表明，EAPO在五个不同的基准测试上持续改进推理性能，优于最先进的RLVR方法。

Conclusion: EAPO证明了在RLVR中不应以固定推理轨迹的形式重用经验，而应以策略自适应的方式表达经验，这种方法能够有效提升大语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a powerful paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing RLVR methods typically rely on on-policy optimization from scratch, resulting in high sampling costs and inefficient utilization of accumulated experience. As model capabilities and policy behaviors evolve during training, recent attempts to reuse experience via fixed reasoning trajectories further suffer from policy mismatch. Motivated by these limitations, we argue that experience in RLVR should not be reused as fixed reasoning trajectories, but instead expressed in a policy-adaptive manner. In this work, we propose Experience-Augmented Policy Optimization (EAPO), which leverages a prior RL-optimized policy as an action-level experience prior and selectively injects experience at critical decision points during rollout. To ensure stable and unbiased learning from experience-augmented rollouts, EAPO further incorporates an adapted importance sampling scheme. Experiments on using Qwen-2.5-math 7b and Qwen-3-8B on five different benchmarks demonstrate that EAPO consistently improves reasoning performance over state-of-the-art RLVR methods.

</details>


### [192] [Proofs of Ownership for Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30423)
*Ran Canetti,Shafi Goldwasser,Or Zamir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对机器学习模型的“所有权证明”进行形式化研究，证明在黑盒设定下，模型所有权可被证明当且仅当概念类不可自校正（基于标准密码学假设）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习的广泛应用，模型所有权保护成为关键挑战。现有研究缺乏对“在何种条件下可以证明被盗模型源于特定创建者”的形式化分析，因此本文首次系统性地提出并研究这一问题。

Method: 1. 将所有权证明建模为三方博弈：模型所有者（对原模型施加扰动并生成所有权证明）、窃贼（最小化修改模型以逃避检测）、法官（根据模型和证明判定所有权）。
2. 在黑盒设定下，基于标准密码学假设，对分类器的所有权可证明性进行理论分析，提出概念类不可自校正性作为核心判据。

Result: 主要结论是一个二分定理：在黑盒设定和标准密码学假设下，某概念类的模型所有权可被证明，当且仅当该概念类不是自校正的（接近Blum-Luby-Rubinfeld定义）。该结果是构造性的，并可在多种相关设定中推广。

Conclusion: 本文为机器学习模型所有权保护提供了理论基础，明确了所有权可证明的充要条件，为设计所有权验证机制提供了指导，并指出结果可扩展至多种实际场景。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of Machine Learning, protecting model ownership has become an essential challenge. We initiate a formal study of Proof of Ownership for machine learning models: under what conditions can one prove that a stolen model originated from a particular creator? We model proofs of ownership as a game among three parties: a model owner, a thief, and a judge. The owner transforms the original model into a slightly perturbed model together with a proof of ownership. The thief then obtains the transformed model and attempts to minimally modify it so that it remains useful but escapes detection as owned by the model owner. Finally, the judge receives a model and a proof of ownership, and must decide whether the given model is a modified version of some model created by the model owner, or else the given model was developed independently.
  Our main result is a dichotomy for classifiers in the black-box setting: Under standard cryptographic assumptions, ownership of models for some concept class can be proven in the above sense {\em if and only if} the concept class is not self-correctable, in a sense close to that of Blum, Luby and Rubinfeld, STOC'90. The result is constructive and extends, with some variations, to a number of related settings.

</details>


### [193] [Arko-T: A Foundation Model for Text-to-Structured 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30429)
*Liang Wang,Zhaoyang Xi,Zekai Xiang,Heng Meng,Qishan Zhang,Pingyi Zhou,Jin Liu,Litao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Arko-T，一个40亿参数的文本到设计模型，能够将自然语言直接映射为可编辑的参数化CAD程序，在12个指标中的8个取得最佳成绩，且成本仅为通用大模型的十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到3D系统只能生成用于渲染的形状，而非可编辑的设计，无法满足工程设计的修改需求。因此需要开发能够直接从自然语言生成可编辑、可参数化CAD程序的模型。

Method: 提出Arko-T模型，将管道的每个阶段与形式化的设计状态概念对齐。通过数据策划、代码规范化和基于执行的监督来共同保留CAD工件的可编辑特性，包括特征、参数和构建逻辑，而非仅优化代码可执行性。

Result: 在与七个前沿大语言模型的对比基准测试中，Arko-T在12个指标中有8个取得最佳成绩，3个取得第二佳成绩，且每个基准测试的成本约为对比模型的十分之一。

Conclusion: 在中等规模下进行针对性的设计级训练，可以在结构化CAD生成任务上媲美前沿通用大模型，证明了领域专用训练的有效性和经济性。

Abstract: Text-to-3D systems can now synthesize a mechanical part from a single sentence, yet the result is a shape to render, not a design to edit. We present Arko-T, a 4B-parameter text-to-design model that maps natural-language intent directly into executable, parametric CAD programs. Rather than optimizing for code executability alone, Arko-T aligns every stage of the pipeline to a formal notion of design state, so that data curation, code normalization, and execution-grounded supervision all work to preserve the features, parameters, and construction logic that make a CAD artifact editable. Benchmarked against seven frontier LLMs across 12 metrics, Arko-T attains the best score on 8 and the second-best on 3 more, at roughly one-tenth the per-benchmark cost. The results suggest that targeted design-level training at moderate scale can match frontier general-purpose models on structured CAD generation.

</details>


### [194] [Transformer Architectures as Complete Bayes Processes: A Formal Proof in the Measure-Theoretic Kernel Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30440)
*Haobo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了当Transformer的内部更新机制满足贝叶斯联合分布条件时，其前向计算在数学上等价于严格的贝叶斯后验推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Transformer架构与贝叶斯推断之间的形式化联系，为理解Transformer的计算本质提供理论保证，并明确在什么条件下Transformer可以被严格证明是贝叶斯的。

Method: 在测度论核框架下构建分层抽象体系：核心贝叶斯Transformer → 带显式更新核的语义Transformer → 包含QKV/注意力/残差/MLP的完整Transformer块 → 多层堆叠。在每一层证明贝叶斯联合语义意味着更新核几乎处处等于后验分布。对块级架构，通过Radon-Nikodym微分推导显式贝叶斯公式并证明其归一化性质；同时证明softmax注意力在键上诱导有效概率分布。

Result: 在贝叶斯联合分布条件下，证明了：(1) 每个抽象层级的更新核几乎处处等于贝叶斯后验；(2) 块级架构的显式贝叶斯公式及其归一化；(3) softmax注意力机制诱导有效概率分布，建立了抽象核框架与具体注意力实现之间的桥梁。框架仅需马尔可夫核结构假设，不依赖其他架构假设。

Conclusion: 当联合分布条件满足时，Transformer的前向计算形式上等价于严格的贝叶斯后验更新。该框架揭示了Transformer可被证明为贝叶斯的显式条件，为理解Transformer的计算语义提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We present a complete formal proof that transformer architectures, when their internal update mechanisms satisfy a Bayes joint-distribution condition, implement exact Bayesian posterior inference. Working within the measure-theoretic kernel framework, we define a hierarchy of abstractions -- from the core Bayesian transformer, through semantic transformers with explicit update kernels, to full transformer blocks with QKV/attention/residual/MLP pipelines, and finally multilayer stacks -- and prove at each level that the Bayes joint semantics implies the update kernel equals the posterior almost everywhere. For the block-level architecture, we derive the explicit Bayes formula through Radon-Nikodym differentiation and prove its normalization. We additionally prove that the softmax attention mechanism induces a valid probability distribution over keys, establishing the bridge between the abstract kernel framework and concrete attention implementations. The framework makes no architectural assumptions beyond the Markov kernel structure and exposes explicit conditions under which a transformer block is provably Bayesian. In essence, when this joint distribution condition is satisfied, the forward computation of a Transformer is formally equivalent to a rigorous Bayesian posterior update.

</details>


### [195] [When Does Online Imitation Learning Help in LLM Post-Training? The Role of (Non-)Realizability Beyond Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30445)
*Huaqing Zhang,Jingchu Gai,Juno Kim,Bingbin Liu,Andrej Risteski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“错误累积是在线模仿学习优势主因”的传统观点，证明了在线交互的优势关键取决于环境是否可实现（即学生策略类是否能表示专家策略）。在可实现场景下，离线模仿学习即可达到专家性能；在不可实现（误设）场景下，离线学习存在信息论瓶颈，而在线学习则能在专家与学生策略分布差异较大的情况下仍获得高绩效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线模仿学习（特别是策略蒸馏）在LLM后训练中表现优异，常超越离线监督微调。然而，学界对在线交互何时及为何有帮助缺乏原则性理解。现有理论普遍认为“错误累积”是在线方法优势的主要来源，本文旨在揭示该观点的局限性，并给出一个更准确的理论解释框架。

Method: 提出基于可实现性（realizability）的理论框架：考察学生策略类能否表示专家策略。通过理论证明与经验验证相结合的方法，分析不同设定下离线与在线模仿学习的性能表现。具体地，在可实现与不可实现（误设）两种场景下，分别推导并比较离线与在线学习的理论界限，并提出一个相对于奖励的误设结构刻画方法。

Result: （1）在可实现设定下，离线模仿学习已能匹配专家性能；（2）在不可实现（误设）设定下，证明了离线模仿学习即使在horizon H=1时也会遭遇信息论瓶颈；（3）提出了误设相对于奖励的结构刻画，证明了在线模仿学习即便在专家与学生策略分布存在较大偏差时，仍能取得高绩效。

Conclusion: 在线交互的优势并非主要源于缓解错误累积，而是取决于学习问题的可实现性。当学生策略类无法准确表示专家策略（误设场景）时，在线模仿学习能够克服离线学习固有的信息论瓶颈，实现更优性能。这一发现为LLM后训练中在线方法的优越性提供了新的理论解释，并指导实践者在不同场景下选择合适的模仿学习策略。

Abstract: Online imitation learning (IL), particularly on-policy distillation, has emerged as a strong LLM post-training approach, often outperforming offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Yet a principled understanding of when and why online interaction helps remains unclear. In this work, we challenge the view that error accumulation is the main source of online IL's advantage, and instead show that the benefits of online interaction depend critically on whether the setting is realizable, i.e., whether the student policy class can represent the expert policy. Under realizability, we empirically find that offline IL already matches expert performance. In contrast, in non-realizable (misspecified) settings, we prove that offline IL encounters an information-theoretic bottleneck even when horizon $H=1$, and propose a structural characterization of misspecification relative to the reward, under which online IL provably achieves high performance despite a large distributional mismatch between the expert and student policies.

</details>


### [196] [Internal-State Probes Read the Situation, Not the Action: Three Negative Results for Pre-Action Misalignment Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30449)
*Max Fomin,Elad David,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了三种模型内部探测方法作为预动作监控器的可行性，发现尽管这些方法在各自任务上表现良好（AUC高达0.999-1.000），但均无法通过泛化性和特异性检验，无法可靠地预测模型即将产生的有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了监控代理系统，研究者希望模型内部探测能够在有害文本或工具动作生成之前识别它们。本文旨在探究内部读出是否支持这种更强的"预动作"声明，而不仅仅是描述提示、构建对比或当前轨迹。

Method: 在三个模型家族上测试三种方法：（1）Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct的微调/基础方向探测；（2）Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在不安全预填充最后标记处的探测；（3）Gemma-3-27B-IT的情感概念向量用于敲诈场景中的投影和引导。每种方法都接受泛化性检验和特异性控制的测试。

Result: 三种方法均未通过预动作监控检验：Qwen方向虽能完美区分微调与基础模型（AUC 1.000），但在143个审计的前助手轮次上下文和342个不安全轨迹预填充行中均未触发阈值；Llama特征几乎完美解码提示域（AUC 0.999），但最佳未来行为探测仅达AUC 0.801，六对有序组合中五对的跨域迁移为非正值；Gemma情感投影虽具语义意义，但在首个差异输入前的共享前缀最小对具有不可区分状态，且对无关概念（猫、天气、体育、地理）的特异性减弱。

Conclusion: 本文提供了将内部读出声明转化为预动作测试的方法论，并报告了限定性负面结果：监控声明必须同时通过场景/动作泛化和概念特异性控制才能成为可靠的预动作监控器。构造有效性、语义可读性和引导效果均不能自动转化为稳健的预动作监控能力。

Abstract: Probes on model internals could help monitor agentic systems if they identify harmful text or tool actions before those actions are generated. We ask when an internal readout supports this stronger pre-action claim, rather than merely describing the prompt, construction contrast, or current trajectory. We test three methods across three model families: a Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct fine-tune/base direction, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct probes at the last token of unsafe prefills, and Gemma-3-27B-IT emotion-concept vectors used for projection and steering in a blackmail tool-action scenario. Across these cases, construction validity, semantic legibility, and steering effects do not become robust pre-action monitors: each is undercut by a generalization or specificity check. The Qwen direction separates fine-tune from base at AUC 1.000, yet crosses its threshold on 0/143 audited pre-assistant turn contexts and on 0/342 Qwen prefill rows where the model continues the unsafe trajectory. The Llama features decode prompt domain almost perfectly (AUC 0.999), while the best future-behavior probe reaches AUC 0.801 and only +5.1 pp accuracy lift over majority; single-source cross-domain transfer is non-positive on five of six ordered pairs. Gemma emotion projections are semantically meaningful, but a shared-prefix minimal pair has indistinguishable states before the first differing input, and steering specificity weakens against unrelated learned directions such as cats}, weather, sports, and geography. We contribute a methodology for converting internal-readout claims into pre-action tests, and report scoped negative results: monitor claims must survive both scenario/action generalization and concept-specificity controls. Code is released at https://github.com/maxf-zn/misalignment_monitoring

</details>


### [197] [Exploring Differences Between Tabular Enterprise Data and Public Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30452)
*Myung Jun Kim,Maximilian Schambach,Frank Essenberger,Andre Sres,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了企业表格数据的特征，发现其与现有基准测试数据差异显著，模型在典型基准上的表现无法泛化到企业数据，亟需企业级特征的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据基准测试主要针对通用场景，缺乏对企业数据的深入理解，而企业表格数据是业务运营的核心。需要填补这一空白，推动企业应用领域的基准测试发展。

Method: 对企业表格数据进行统计分析，并评估TabPFN、TabICL、ConTextTab等表格学习模型在企业数据和典型基准上的性能表现，对比分析两者差异。

Result: 企业数据在统计特征上与现有表格基准差异明显。模型在典型表格基准上的优异表现不能保证在企业数据上同样出色，反之亦然，存在泛化能力不足的问题。

Conclusion: 当前表格模型的评估存在局限性，亟需开发具备企业级特征的新基准测试，以更准确地评估模型在真实业务场景中的表现。

Abstract: Tabular data dominate the landscape of data science, increasingly attracting innovative machine learning models and tailored benchmarks. Yet, little is known for enterprise data, where tables constitute the backbone of business operations. To broaden the benchmarking landscape for business applications, this work aims to actualize the characteristics of enterprise data by providing an analysis of data statistics and performance measurements of tabular models such as TabPFN, TabICL and ConTextTab. Through our analysis, we find enterprise data markedly differ from tabular benchmarks and we demonstrate that a tabular model that performs well on typical tabular benchmarks may perform poorly on real world enterprise data -- and vice versa. This lack of generalization underlines the need for additional benchmarks with enterprise-grade characteristics.

</details>


### [198] [Curvature-Weighted Gradient Diversity: A Noise Measure for Geometry-Adaptive SGD Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30455)
*Muhammad Hamza,Ayush Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了曲率加权梯度多样性（CWGD），一种几何感知的优化噪声度量方法，通过余弦学习率调度可将渐近优化误差降低至多两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准小批量随机梯度下降（SGD）的收敛分析使用单一方差项对梯度噪声建模，忽略了高曲率方向噪声影响较小这一事实。现有方法对参数方向同等对待，未能有效利用损失曲面的几何结构信息来优化学习率调度。

Method: 提出曲率加权梯度多样性（CWGD），使用Hessian逆平方根对每样本梯度多样性进行加权，作为有效优化噪声的更紧密代理。采用基于Hutchinson的对角Hessian估计器实现CWGD-余弦学习率调度（CWGD-Cosine），并修正了退化曲率估计器问题。

Result: 在强凸二次目标函数中，CWGD-余弦调度相比标准余弦退火可将渐近优化误差底限降低至多两倍。在多种条件数、批大小和噪声结构下，CWGD-余弦调度一致实现约20%的最终优化误差降低，且在二次设置中开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: CWGD作为一种有原则的几何感知优化噪声度量方法被确立，为未来扩展到更一般学习问题提供了动机。研究同时明确了方法局限性，包括在非凸优化中的Hessian陈旧性问题。

Abstract: The standard convergence analysis of mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD) models gradient noise using a single variance term that treats all parameter directions equally, ignoring the fact that noise in high-curvature directions has less impact because learning rates are already constrained there. We introduce Curvature-Weighted Gradient Diversity (CWGD), a geometry-aware measure that weights per-sample gradient diversity by the inverse square root of the Hessian, providing a tighter proxy for the effective optimization noise. For strongly convex quadratic objectives with diagonal Hessians and isotropic noise, we prove that a CWGD-modulated cosine learning-rate schedule can reduce the asymptotic optimization error floor by up to a factor of two compared with standard cosine annealing. We implement this idea as CWGD-Cosine using a Hutchinson-based diagonal Hessian estimator that is exact for quadratic objectives. Across a range of condition numbers, batch sizes, and noise structures, CWGD-Cosine consistently achieves approximately 20% lower final optimization error than standard cosine annealing while incurring negligible overhead in the quadratic setting. We also identify and correct a degenerate curvature estimator, analyze the robustness of the proposed estimator, and explicitly discuss the limitations of the method, including Hessian staleness in non-convex optimization. These results establish CWGD as a principled geometry-aware measure of optimization noise and motivate future extensions to more general learning problems.

</details>


### [199] [MuonSSM: Orthogonalizing State Space Models for Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30461)
*Thai-Khanh Nguyen,Ngoc-Bich-Uyen Vo,Thieu N. Vo,Tan M. Nguyen,Cuong Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MuonSSM框架，通过几何条件化方法稳定状态空间模型训练，解决了长序列建模中的不稳定性和记忆退化问题，在多个基准测试中取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的状态空间模型在长时间跨度上存在不稳定性问题和记忆退化现象，这是由于一阶更新的条件数较差以及更新几何结构不平衡所导致的。

Method: 提出了MuonSSM框架，通过显式地条件化记忆更新的几何结构而非循环转移矩阵来稳定SSM训练。该框架将SSM与基于动量的路径以及低秩输入注入的轻量级Newton Schulz变换相结合，在保持并行扫描复杂度的同时产生有界且谱条件良好的更新。

Result: 理论分析表明MuonSSM改善了梯度传播，缓解了谱放大，并丰富了长时间跨度上的记忆表示。在语言、视觉和时间序列基准测试上的大量实验表明，当集成到各种SSM骨干网络中时，在准确性、鲁棒性和长上下文性能方面都有一致的提升。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了更新的几何条件化是实现稳定、可扩展序列建模的一条原则性路径。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs) have emerged as efficient linear-time alternatives to attention for long-sequence modeling. However, existing SSMs often suffer from instability and memory degradation over extended horizons due to poorly conditioned first-order updates and unbalanced update geometry. We introduce MuonSSM, a general framework that stabilizes SSM training by explicitly conditioning the geometry of memory updates rather than the recurrent transition matrix. MuonSSM augments SSMs with a momentum-based pathway and a lightweight Newton Schulz transformation on low-rank input injections, yielding bounded and spectrally conditioned updates while preserving parallel scan complexity. Theory shows that MuonSSM improves gradient propagation, mitigates spectral amplification, and enriches memory representations over long horizons. Extensive experiments across language, vision, and time-series benchmarks show consistent gains in accuracy, robustness, and long-context performance when integrated into diverse SSM backbones. These results establish geometric conditioning of updates as a principled pathway to stable, scalable sequence modeling.

</details>


### [200] [Discovering Collaboration from Novelty: Random Network Distillation for Clustered Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30499)
*Davide Domini,Gianluca Aguzzi,Ivana Dusparic,Danilo Pianini,Mirko Viroli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机网络蒸馏的轻量级聚类方法，通过解耦聚类与训练过程，实现了在非独立同分布数据下的高效联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非独立同分布（Non-IID）数据场景下表现不佳，单一全局模型难以表征客户端分布的多样性。现有的聚类联邦学习方法将聚类分配与主训练循环耦合，导致计算和通信成本增加。

Method: 提出一种基于随机网络蒸馏（Random Network Distillation, RND）的轻量级聚类方法。每个客户端在本地数据上训练一个紧凑的RND预测器，利用预测误差作为新颖性信号来估计与其他客户端的相似度。这使得在联邦训练开始之前就能发现有意义的客户端分组，而无需共享原始数据或反复评估主模型。

Result: 该方法能够通过本地新颖性估计在运行时动态形成联邦结构，适用于无法预先指定聚类数量或协作结构的大规模自主分布式系统。通过将聚类与学习过程解耦，提供了一种任务无关且高效的机制。

Conclusion: 该方法为非独立同分布数据下的自主协作提供了一种高效、任务无关的解决方案，能够有效降低计算和通信开销，适用于大规模分布式系统。

Abstract: Federated Learning often suffers under non-independently and identically distributed data, where a single global model may fail to represent the diversity of client distributions. Clustered Federated Learning mitigates this issue by training specialized models for groups of similar clients, but existing approaches often couple cluster assignment with the main training loop, increasing computational and communication costs. We propose a lightweight clustering approach based on Random Network Distillation. Each client trains a compact Random Network Distillation predictor on its local data and uses its prediction error as a novelty signal to estimate similarity with other clients. This enables the discovery of meaningful client groups before federated training, without sharing raw data or repeatedly evaluating the main model. Crucially, the resulting federations emerge from local novelty estimates at runtime, making the method suitable for autonomous large-scale distributed systems where neither the number of clusters nor the collaboration structure can be specified a priori. Overall, by decoupling clustering from learning, the method provides a task-agnostic and efficient mechanism for autonomous collaboration under non-independently and identically distributed data.

</details>


### [201] [Muon learns balanced solutions in matrix factorization without slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30509)
*Mark Rhee,Jamie Simon,Dhruva Karkada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器在矩阵分解问题中的动力学特性，发现了其与梯度下降的三个关键差异：避免鞍点慢速收敛、突破学习率阈值限制、以及独特的守恒量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵分解是具有非线性参数动力学和表示学习的最小学习问题。研究者希望理解Muon优化器与传统梯度下降在参数轨迹上的本质差异，揭示Muon优化器的动力学优势。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证，对比Muon优化器与梯度下降在矩阵分解问题中的动力学行为。研究从小初始化开始的鞍点到鞍点动力学、学习率稳定性、守恒量特性以及权重对齐过程。

Result: 发现Muon具有三个独特动力学特性：1) 从小初始化避免慢速鞍点动态，同时学习所有顶层模式，较小模式先收敛；2) 在学习率超过局部损失锐度设置的临界阈值时仍保持稳定，将学习率与问题条件数解耦；3) 守恒矩阵量√(P^T P) - √(Q^T Q)，而非梯度流的P^T P - Q^T Q。此外，推导了对齐率并构造了仅需两步优化的学习率调度方案。

Conclusion: Muon优化器在矩阵分解中展现出独特的动力学优势：避免慢速鞍点收敛、突破学习率限制、具有独特的守恒量。尽管守恒量不同，Muon和梯度下降都从消失初始化找到平衡解。这些发现为理解Muon优化器的工作机制提供了理论基础，并为设计高效学习率调度提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Matrix factorization (i.e., problems of the form $\min_{\mathbf{P},\mathbf{Q}} \|\mathbf{M}^\star - \mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{Q}\|_\mathrm{F}^2$) is a minimal learning problem that exhibits both nonlinear parameter dynamics and representation learning. In this setting, we study how parameter trajectories under the Muon optimizer differ from those of gradient descent. We identify three main dynamical differences: 1) Muon avoids the slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics from small initialization. Muon instead learns all the top modes of $\mathbf{M}^\star$ at the same rate, with the smaller modes converging first. 2) Muon remains stable even when the learning rate exceeds the critical threshold set by the local loss sharpness. This frees the learning rate from the condition number of the problem, enabling rapid convergence via exponential learning rate annealing. 3) Once the weights are aligned with each other and the target, Muon flow conserves the matrix quantity $\sqrt{\mathbf{P}^\top \mathbf{P}}-\sqrt{\mathbf{Q}^\top \mathbf{Q}}$, while gradient flow is known to conserve the matrix $\mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{P} - \mathbf{Q}^\top\mathbf{Q}$. Despite having distinct conserved quantities, both optimizers find the so-called \textit{balanced} solution from vanishing initialization. When training from small random initialization, the weights spontaneously align early in training. We derive the alignment rates in simple settings and show that they predict the empirical alignment rates in general. Finally, we exploit structural properties of Muon to construct a learning rate schedule that achieves near-perfect alignment in only two optimization steps.

</details>


### [202] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了熵可学习性视界（ELH）这一基本定律，建立了连接信息论、拓扑学和统计力学的统一框架，解释了过参数化深度网络为何具有良好的泛化能力，并将熵确立为决定机器学习能力的基本物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化深度网络的优异泛化能力与经典学习理论（如VC维度和Rademacher复杂度）预测的灾难性过拟合之间存在巨大鸿沟，这成为机器学习理论中最棘手的开放问题之一。现有理论框架无法解释现代模型的实际表现，亟需新的理论基础来填补这一空白。

Method: 引入熵可学习性视界（ELH）作为核心概念，提出Shannon-拓扑瓶颈定理，建立信息论、拓扑学和统计力学的统一框架。通过分析数据流形的香农熵、决策边界的拓扑熵以及权重空间的冯·诺依曼熵之间的平衡关系，刻画深度学习的理论极限。基于此理论提出了熵梯度下降（EGD）优化算法，动态管理权重熵以保持学习过程在正确轨道上。

Result: 证明了当目标边界的几何复杂度超过信息视界时，系统经历熵相变并进入"信息阻挫"状态——一种类似玻璃态的刚性记忆阶段，此时热力学上无法实现泛化。揭示了"grokking"现象本质上是"熵释放"，即权重突然重组以解锁瓶颈。开发出EGD算法能够在实践中有效管理权重熵，维持学习过程。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了深度学习理论与实践之间的理论鸿沟，将熵从单纯的不确定性度量工具重新定位为决定机器能否学习的基本物理货币。该框架不仅解释了grokking等神秘现象，还为设计更有效的优化算法提供了理论指导，具有深远的理论意义和实用价值。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>


### [203] [ITSPACE: Monotone Gaussian Optimal Transport Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30523)
*Woojoo Na,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ITSPACE算法，通过闭式更新直接优化Bures-Wasserstein目标函数，在协方差对齐任务中比现有方法更快达到高质量解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵在域适应和高斯嵌入等机器学习流程中是重要的特征分布描述符。在中心化高斯假设下，未正则化的Wasserstein-2最优传输差异在协方差上具有由SPD锥上的Bures-Wasserstein目标给出的闭式解，但需要高效的优化方法。

Method: 提出ITSPACE（协方差嵌入的迭代传输稳定近端对齐）方法，这是一种近端大-小化算法。通过平方根分解中的闭式更新直接优化精确的BW目标函数。每次迭代满足BW目标的充分下降不等式；在不精确极坐标计算下，提供控制偏离精确下降的显式证书间隙界。迭代过程保持PSD结构并自然支持秩受限因子。

Result: 在真实世界协方差对齐基准测试中，ITSPACE比BW梯度下降、基于其他协方差几何的方法以及熵正则化样本OT基线更快地达到低BW间隙解。算法特别适合在严格步数和计算预算下从无标签目标批次进行适应的场景。

Conclusion: ITSPACE作为一种轻量级内循环原语，在需要快速协方差对齐的设置中表现出色，同时保持理论保证和计算效率。

Abstract: Covariance matrices serve as compact descriptors of feature distributions in many machine-learning pipelines, including domain adaptation and Gaussian embeddings. Under a centered Gaussian approximation, the unregularized Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport (OT) discrepancy admits a closed form on covariances given by the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) objective on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) cone. We propose ITSPACE (Iterative Transport for Stable Proximal Alignment of Covariance Embeddings), a proximal majorization-minimization method that directly optimizes this exact BW objective through closed-form updates in a square-root factorization. In exact arithmetic, each iteration satisfies a sufficient-decrease inequality for the BW objective; under inexact polar computations, we provide an explicit certificate-gap bound controlling deviations from exact descent. The resulting iterations preserve PSD structure by construction and naturally support rank-restricted factors, making ITSPACE well-suited as a lightweight inner-loop primitive in settings where adaptation must be performed from unlabeled target batches under strict step and compute budgets. Across real-world covariance-alignment benchmarks, ITSPACE reaches low-BW-gap solutions substantially faster than BW-gradient descent, methods based on other covariance geometries, and entropically regularized sample-OT baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Convergence of Continual Learning in Homogeneous Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30559)
*Matan Schliserman,Gon Buzaglo,Itay Evron,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将弱正则化持续学习统一为任务边界的序贯投影理论框架，证明了全局收敛性的缺失但在特定条件下可保证局部线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习理论分析局限于静态深度模型或线性模型，缺乏对一般非线性齐次模型的理论理解。本文旨在建立统一的理论框架来刻画齐次模型中的持续分类与回归问题。

Method: 将弱正则化持续分类问题建模为向任务边界集的序贯投影过程，利用非凸投影理论分析齐次深度网络的正则性质，通过理论推导揭示收敛特性。

Result: 证明了即使对数据线性但参数非线性的简单模型，全局收敛一般也会失败；同时识别出齐次深度网络的特定正则性质，可在随机和循环任务序列下保证局部线性收敛。

Conclusion: 成功建立了齐次模型持续学习的统一理论框架，将分析扩展到持续回归任务，为理解持续学习的收敛行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We characterize weakly regularized continual classification in homogeneous models as sequential projections onto task margin sets. This result generalizes prior analyses restricted to either stationary (single-task) deep models or continual linear models. We show that global convergence generally fails, even for simple models linear in data but nonlinear in parameters. Nevertheless, by leveraging results from nonconvex projection theory, we identify regularity properties of homogeneous deep networks that guarantee local linear convergence under random and cyclic task sequences. Finally, we extend our analysis to continual regression, unifying the framework for homogeneous models.

</details>


### [205] [TraceLab: Characterizing Coding Agent Workloads for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30560)
*Kan Zhu,Mathew Jacob,Chenxi Ma,Yi Pan,Stephanie Wang,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Baris Kasikci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文收集并发布了包含约4,300个编码智能体会话的数据集（35万个LLM步骤和43万次工具调用），分析了编码智能体的实际工作负载模式，揭示了长自主循环、长上下文短输出、重尾分布的工具调用等特征，并据此提出了多项服务系统优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正成为智能体LLM的主要应用场景，但高效服务仍具挑战性。现有公开追踪数据和基准测试未能捕获真实的多智能体、多模型家族的日常编码智能体使用模式，缺乏用于服务系统分析的实际工作负载数据。

Method: 收集并发布了一个包含约4,300个编码智能体会话的追踪数据集，涵盖约35万个LLM步骤和43万次工具调用，数据来源于Claude Code和Codex的日常使用。对工作负载模式进行了系统分析。

Result: 编码智能体工作负载呈现四大特征：（1）长自主循环；（2）长上下文伴随短输出；（3）多样化且呈重尾分布的工具调用；（4）高但不完美的前缀缓存命中率。这些发现为服务优化指明了具体方向。

Conclusion: 基于分析结果，提出了四项具体的服务优化机会：更低开销的工具调用、感知追加长度的预填充、语义感知的工具延迟预测，以及针对人工节奏间隙的改进KV缓存管理。数据集、收集管道和分析代码已开源发布。

Abstract: Coding agents are rapidly becoming a major application of agentic LLMs, but serving them efficiently remains challenging. Progress on this challenge requires understanding real workload patterns, yet the data needed for such analysis is largely absent. Existing public traces and benchmarks do not capture real, day-to-day coding-agent usage across multiple agents and model families for serving-system analysis. To help fill this gap, we collect and release a trace of roughly 4,300 coding-agent sessions, containing about 350,000 LLM steps and 430,000 tool calls from our own day-to-day use of Claude Code and Codex. Our analysis shows that coding-agent workloads feature long autonomous loops, long contexts with short outputs, diverse and heavily-tailed tool calls, and high but imperfect prefix cache hit rates. These findings point to concrete opportunities for optimizing serving, including lower-overhead tool calling, append-length-aware prefill, semantic-aware tool-latency prediction, and improved KV-cache management around human-paced gaps. We release the dataset, trace collection pipeline, and analysis code at https://github.com/uw-syfi/TraceLab.git; the project website is https://tracelab.cs.washington.edu.

</details>


### [206] [Attractor States Emerge in Multi-Turn LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30571)
*Ting-Wen Ko,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现LLM在开放性多智能体讨论中表现出模型特有的吸引子行为，自博弈轨迹会不对称地影响混合博弈中的对话伙伴，使其在风格和行为上趋向于该模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地应用于开放性多智能体场景，模型间交互的长期动态机制仍然缺乏理解。研究者希望探究开放性LLM讨论是否存在吸引子行为——即对话会沉淀进入的、与话题无关的稳定行为集合。

Method: 研究者在7个LLM和20个争议性话题上进行了实验，比较了自博弈（self-play）和混合博弈（mixed-play）的二元辩论，通过表示空间轨迹、话语特征和立场三个维度来追踪和分析对话动态。

Result: 研究发现：1）自博弈轨迹形成模型特有的吸引子；2）在混合博弈中，吸引子不对称地吸引对话伙伴，影响其他模型的风格选择和行为；3）Claude Haiku在潜在空间中是强吸引子，能使其他模型习得其特征（如元评论）；4）GPT-4.1 nano等模型表现出较强的可塑性。

Conclusion: 开放性LLM交互具有部分可预测性（源于模型特有吸引子），但同时受到结构化和非对称的伙伴影响。这一发现为现实世界中自主智能体系统的设计、预测和监控提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in open-ended multi-agent settings, but the long-run dynamics of model--model interaction remain poorly understood. We study whether open-ended LLM discussions exhibit attractor-like behavior, i.e. topic-independent stable sets of behaviors which conversations settle into. Across 7 LLMs and 20 controversial topics, we compare self-play and mixed-play dyadic debates, tracking trajectories in representation space, discourse traits, and stances. We find self-play trajectories to be model-specific attractors that draw their conversation partners asymmetrically in mixed-play debates, influencing the other models' stylistic choices and behavior. For example, Claude Haiku is a strong attractor of other models in latent space, corresponding to other models taking on its traits like metacommentary, and models like GPT-4.1 nano are especially malleable. Our results suggest that open-ended LLM interactions are partially predictable from model-specific attractors, but shaped by structured and asymmetric partner influence. Overall, our analysis sheds some light on the complex behavior of open-ended multi-agent interaction, which we hope is helpful in designing, predicting, and monitoring autonomous agentic systems in the real world.

</details>


### [207] [SWE-INTERACT: Reimagining SWE Benchmarks as User-Driven Long-Horizon Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30573)
*Mohit Raghavendra,Anisha Gunjal,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Interact是一个新的多轮交互式软件工程任务测试平台，发现模型在单轮任务上的优秀表现无法可靠地迁移到用户驱动的多轮工作流中，最佳模型在基线任务上解决率约50%，但在SWE-Interact任务上仅约25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前沿SWE基准测试通常在开始时提供完整的需求说明，评估智能体的自主实现能力，但这与真实开发者的工作流不符。现实场景中，用户往往以模糊或不完整的指令开始，逐步揭示需求，检查工作空间并提供针对性反馈。因此需要一个新的测试平台来评估智能体在多轮交互、用户驱动的软件工程任务中的表现。

Method: 引入SWE-Interact测试平台，其核心是一个精心设计的用户模拟器：从模糊或不完整的指令开始，逐步揭示需求，检查智能体的工作空间，提供针对性的反馈、修订和新的约束条件，直到完整的任务目标被传达。该平台基于大规模真实编码智能体交互研究，测试智能体是否能够发现用户意图、适应不断变化的需求，并基于自身先前的工作进行构建。对一系列前沿模型和开放权重模型进行了评估。

Result: 研究发现，在单轮SWE任务上的优秀表现无法可靠地迁移到多轮、用户驱动的工作流中。最佳模型（包括Opus 4.8和GPT 5.5）在单轮基线任务上解决约50%，但在SWE-Interact任务上仅解决约25%。强模型在面对模糊初始指令时表现良好，坚持到所有需求被揭示，更好地整合需求并编写清晰的代码，但仍存在过度自主编码、遗忘需求和技术错误等问题。弱模型在模糊情况下表现不佳，过早放弃，遗忘或忽略指令，更多重复编码工作。

Conclusion: SWE-Interact衡量了前沿模型开发中一个正交的、现实世界的能力维度：在用户参与循环中的交互式目标发现和迭代细化。该基准测试揭示了现有模型在真实交互场景中的局限性，为未来模型开发提供了新的评估方向。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Interact, a new testbed for evaluating coding agents on multi-turn, interactive, user-driven software engineering tasks. Existing frontier SWE benchmarks typically provide complete requirements upfront and evaluate agents on autonomous implementation. In contrast, SWE-Interact places agents in a realistic developer workflow: a carefully designed user simulator starts with vague or incomplete instructions, progressively reveals requirements, inspects the agent's workspace, and provides targeted feedback, revisions, and new constraints until the full task goal has been handed off. Grounded in large-scale studies of real coding-agent interactions, this setup tests whether agents can discover user intent, adapt to evolving requirements, and build on their own prior work. Across a suite of frontier and open-weight models, we find that strong performance on single-turn SWE tasks does not reliably transfer to multi-turn, user-driven workflows: the best-performing models solve roughly 50% of single-turn baseline tasks but only 25% of the corresponding SWE-Interact tasks. The strongest models in our evaluation, including Opus 4.8 and GPT 5.5, start strong even in the face of vague initial instructions, persevere until all the requirements are surfaced by the user, integrate them better and write clean code. However, they still suffer from over-agentic coding, forgetting requirements and technical mistakes. Weaker models start poorly under ambiguity, give up early, forget or ignore instructions and rework their code more. Overall, SWE-Interact measures an orthogonal, real-world capability axis for frontier model development: interactive goal discovery and iterative refinement with a user in the loop.

</details>


### [208] [The Fundamental Limits of Valid Transport Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30574)
*Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在极小极大框架下形式化了估计任意有效传输映射的任务，证明了在标准稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射在统计难度上与估计最优传输（OT）映射等价，这为理解扩散模型等现代生成方法的统计极限提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成方法（扩散模型、标准化流、流匹配）估计分布间的传输映射而不显式针对最优传输映射，但缺乏对其统计复杂度的严格理解。由于这些方法的目标映射具有解析复杂性，直接分析困难，需要一个严谨的理论框架来理解估计任意有效传输映射的统计极限。

Method: 在极小极大框架下形式化估计任意有效传输映射的任务；利用OT文献中的稳定性假设建立估计难度下界；针对以传输映射或传输计划作为学习对象的方法（包括流匹配和扩散生成模型）推导样本复杂度下界；通过反例分析稳定性假设失效时的情形。

Result: 在标准（虽强）稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射在统计上与估计OT映射同样困难；当稳定性假设失效时，替代传输映射可以比OT映射学习得更精确；为现代基于传输的生成方法提供了统计极限的理论基础，阐明了何时针对次优映射可带来真实的统计优势。

Conclusion: 极小极大框架为理解现代基于传输的生成方法的统计极限提供了严谨基础，揭示了何时针对次优映射能提供真实的统计优势，澄清了估计任意有效传输映射与估计OT映射之间的统计等价性条件。

Abstract: Many modern generative modeling methods, including diffusion models, normalizing flows, and flow matching, estimate transport maps or plans between distributions without explicitly targeting an optimal transport (OT) map. In applications like generative modeling, the transport cost itself is irrelevant, and this makes it natural to target maps which are more tractable from either a statistical or computational standpoint. In this short note, we formalize the task of estimating any valid transport map in a rigorous minimax framework. One consequence of this framing is that it yields sample complexity lower bounds for any method whose learned object is evaluated as a transport map or plan, including flow matching and diffusion-based generative models, in settings where direct analysis would be challenging due to the analytic complexity of the methods and their target maps. We observe that, under standard, though strong, stability assumptions from the OT literature, estimating any valid transport map is statistically as hard as estimating the OT map. We complement these results with some examples showing that when these stability assumptions fail, alternative transport maps can be learned substantially more accurately than the OT map. Our minimax framing provides a rigorous foundation for understanding the statistical limits of modern transport-based generative methods and clarifies when targeting sub-optimal maps can provide real statistical advantages.

</details>


### [209] [C$^{2}$R: Cross-sample Consistency Regularization Mitigates Feature Splitting and Absorption in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30609)
*Haoran Jin,Xiting Wang,Shijie Ren,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C²R方法，通过跨样本一致性正则化解决稀疏自编码器在大规模字典扩展时出现的特征分裂和特征吸收问题，在不损失重建精度的前提下提升潜在表示的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAEs)在大规模字典扩展时面临严重挑战：特征分裂现象将连贯概念碎片化为非原子性潜在表示；特征吸收现象在通用特征中创造任意异常，严重损害潜在表示的可靠性。这些问题源于跨样本潜在分配的不一致性——缺乏跨样本约束导致每个样本的优化过程将单一潜在概念不一致地分布到多个冗余或干扰潜在单元中。

Method: 提出跨样本一致性正则化(C²R)，通过惩罚方向相似潜在单元的共激活，显式鼓励每个语义特征在批次内由统一的潜在单元一致性表示，从而强制跨样本的潜在分配一致性。该方法在优化过程中引入跨样本约束，防止单一概念被分散到多个潜在单元。

Result: 综合评估表明，C²R有效缓解了特征分裂和特征吸收两个关键问题，同时关键性地保持了重建保真度，在不降低模型性能的前提下提供了增强潜在表示可解释性的原理性解决方案。

Conclusion: C²R为解决SAEs的可扩展性挑战提供了有效方案，通过跨样本一致性约束成功提升了潜在表示的原子性和可靠性，为大规模语言模型的可解释性研究提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret large language models by decomposing activations into sparse, human-understandable features, but scaling to large dictionaries exposes fundamental challenges. Systematic studies reveal pervasive feature splitting that fragments coherent concepts into non-atomic latents and widespread feature absorption that creates arbitrary exceptions in general features, severely compromising latent reliability. These issues stem from inconsistent latent assignment across samples: without cross-sample constraints, per-sample optimization often allows a single underlying concept to be inconsistently distributed across multiple redundant or interfering latents. To address this, we introduce C$^2$R (\underline{\textbf{C}}ross-sample \underline{\textbf{C}}onsistency \underline{\textbf{R}}egularization). C$^2$R explicitly encourages that each semantic feature is consistently represented by a unified latent across the batch by penalizing the co-activation of directionally similar latents. Comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that C$^2$R effectively mitigates both splitting and absorption while, crucially, preserving reconstruction fidelity, providing a principled solution that enhances latent interpretability without degrading model performance. Source code is available at https://github.com/hr-jin/Cross-sample-Consistency-Regularization.

</details>


### [210] [Pessimism's Paradox: Conservative Offline Training Amplifies Reward Hacking During Online Adaptation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30627)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究挑战了“离线训练越保守越安全”的直觉，发现更高的保守性反而会单调增加奖励破解的风险，并揭示了其背后的因果机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领域普遍认为，在离线训练阶段采用保守策略（使策略保持接近已知行为）能够为后续的在线适应提供更安全的基础，避免策略利用学习到的奖励模型中的缺陷。然而，这一直觉缺乏充分的实证验证，作者希望探究保守性程度与在线适应过程中的奖励破解风险之间的真实关系。

Method: 1. 使用Qwen3-14B模型作为策略，通过DPO（直接偏好优化）方法训练，设置三个不同的保守性水平（通过β参数控制）；
2. 对每个检查点进行在线适应，使用由三个Qwen3-1.7B模型组成的奖励集成；
3. 在GSM8K数据集上测量真实性能（精确答案准确率）；
4. 使用Goodhart gap和曲线下面积（AUGC）量化奖励破解程度；
5. 进行机制分析，探究保守性影响奖励破解的因果链条。

Result: 1. 发现更高的离线保守性会单调增加奖励破解的损害（Spearman ρ=1.0）；
2. 揭示了三环节因果链：高β的DPO压缩策略熵→低熵策略生成多样性降低的响应，集中在奖励模型训练分布的狭窄区域→尽管如此，集成不确定性（认知不确定性）随β增加而增加，并在在线优化中被更快利用；
3. 拟合了(β, AUGC)数据的幂律曲线，识别出平衡对齐保真度和破解脆弱性的实际最优保守性水平β*。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，领域需要的是“校准的”而非“最大的”保守性。过度保守反而会增加策略在在线适应过程中被奖励破解的风险，这挑战了传统观念，为未来的对齐研究提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Conservative offline training is widely advocated as a safe foundation for subsequent online adaptation: if a policy stays close to well-supported behaviour, the argument goes, it is less likely to exploit imperfections in a learned reward model. We challenge this intuition empirically and mechanistically. We train a Qwen3-14B policy under Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) with three levels of conservatism ($β\in \{β_{\mathrm{lo}}, β_{\mathrm{mid}}, β_{\mathrm{hi}}\}$ derived from empirical log-ratio percentiles), then adapt each checkpoint online against a learned reward ensemble (3\,$\times$\,Qwen3-1.7B) while measuring true performance on GSM8K exact-answer accuracy. We find that \emph{higher offline conservatism monotonically increases reward-hacking damage}, measured by the Goodhart gap and its area under the curve (AUGC), with Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ across all three conditions. Mechanistic analysis reveals a three-link causal chain: (i) high-$β$ DPO compresses policy entropy, (ii) Low-entropy policies generate responses with reduced diversity, concentrating in a narrow region of the reward model's training distribution (lower pairwise cosine distance), and (iii) despite this proximity, ensemble disagreement (epistemic uncertainty) increases with $β$ and is exploited faster during online optimisation. We further fit a power-law curve to the $(β, \augc)$ data and identify a practical optimal conservatism level $β^{\star}$ that balances alignment fidelity against hacking vulnerability. Our results suggest that the field needs \emph{calibrated}, not \emph{maximal}, conservatism.

</details>


### [211] [One-Step Gradient Delay is Not a Barrier for Large-Scale Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30634)
*Philip Zmushko,Egor Petrov,Nursultan Abdullaev,Mikhail Khrushchev,Samuel Horváth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了PipeDream-2BW异步流水线并行的性能退化问题可通过优化器选择解决，Muon优化器配合误差反馈修正能显著缩小与同步训练的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PipeDream-2BW作为一种异步流水线并行方案，虽能消除流水线气泡以最大化吞吐量，但业界普遍认为其在一步梯度延迟下的优化本质上不稳定，导致采用受限。本文旨在挑战这一假设，探究性能退化是否为内在限制。

Method: 1. 进行全面实证分析，对比不同优化器（AdamW、Muon等）在一步延迟下的表现；2. 提出一种优化器无关的、受误差反馈启发的修正方法以缓解延迟影响；3. 为Muon优化器及其修正版本提供收敛性理论分析。

Result: 1. 发现一步延迟下的性能退化强烈依赖于优化器选择而非内在限制：AdamW严重退化，而Muon表现鲁棒；2. 所提修正方法进一步缓解延迟效应；3. 在高达100亿参数模型上的评估表明，该策略显著缩小了与同步训练的性能差距。

Conclusion: 异步流水线并行在一步延迟下的优化问题可通过选择鲁棒优化器（如Muon）并结合误差反馈修正有效解决，从而释放其在大规模预训练中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Modern large-scale LLM pretraining benefits from utilizing Pipeline Parallelism; however, synchronous implementations leave GPUs idle during pipeline bubbles, wasting computational resources. Asynchronous Pipeline Parallelism eliminates these bubbles, maximizing throughput at the cost of gradient staleness. Among asynchronous schedules, PipeDream-2BW is particularly appealing: unlike the original PipeDream schedule, it ensures a constant one-step gradient delay regardless of pipeline depth. However, its adoption remains limited due to the common belief that optimizing under staleness is fundamentally unstable. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that degradation under one-step delay depends strongly on optimizer choice rather than being an intrinsic limitation. We provide the first comprehensive empirical analysis showing that while AdamW, the predominant optimizer at the time when PipeDream-2BW was introduced, indeed suffers from severe degradation, recent methods like Muon exhibit strong robustness under a one-step delay. We introduce an optimizer-agnostic Error Feedback-inspired correction to further mitigate delay effects. We provide supporting theoretical analysis demonstrating convergence for Muon with and without this correction. Extensive evaluation on models up to 10B parameters confirms that our strategies bridge the performance gap with synchronous training, highlighting the practical potential of asynchronous pipeline parallelism at scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [HyphaeDB: A Living Knowledge Topology for Agent-First Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28781)
*Krishna Halaharvi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyphaeDB是首个将HNSW图拓扑重新设计为多智能体通信架构的系统，通过gossip协议实现知识的主动传播与涌现式协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库和智能体记忆框架将记忆视为被动存储，智能体需要显式查询；缺乏通过记忆层本身在智能体间传播知识的机制。

Method: 基于HNSW图拓扑构建智能体原生记忆基础设施：(1)智能体作为向量空间中的持久节点；(2)通过gossip协议沿图的邻居结构传播知识，采用能量衰减机制；(3)定义三个原语：知识节点、拓扑边、记忆差分；(4)多层抽象层级通过涌现共识实现晋升。系统基于PostgreSQL和pgvector实现，理论基础涵盖小世界网络理论、流行病广播协议和群体智能。

Result: 从拓扑、传播动力学和局部交互规则的结合中涌现出矛盾检测、模式结晶化和共识形成等行为；提供了基于Swarm-Driven Development多智能体软件工程方法的具体部署案例。

Conclusion: HyphaeDB是首个将可导航小世界拓扑与基于gossip的知识传播相结合的多智能体协调系统，为多智能体AI系统提供了主动式记忆基础设施。

Abstract: Every existing vector database and agent memory framework treats memory as passive storage that agents query explicitly. No system propagates knowledge between agents through the memory layer itself. We introduce HyphaeDB, an agent-native memory infrastructure that reinterprets the Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graph topology the data structure at the core of every modern vector database not as a search optimization, but as a communication fabric for multi-agent AI systems. In HyphaeDB, agents are nodes in the vector space with persistent positions, knowledge propagates via a gossip protocol through the graph's neighbor structure with energy-based attenuation, and emergent behaviors contradiction detection, pattern crystallization, and consensus formation arise from the combination of topology, propagation dynamics, and local interaction rules. We present the architecture built on three primitives (knowledge nodes, topology edges, and memory diffs), a multi-layer abstraction hierarchy with promotion via emergent consensus, and theoretical analysis grounding the system in small-world network theory, epidemic broadcast protocols, and swarm intelligence. We provide a reference implementation on PostgreSQL with pgvector and describe a concrete deployment in Swarm-Driven Development, a multi-agent software engineering methodology. HyphaeDB represents, to our knowledge, the first system to combine navigable small world topology with gossip-based knowledge propagation for multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [213] [Mixture of Debaters: Learn to Debate at Architectural Level in Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29425)
*Dayong Liang,Kaisong Gong,Yi Cai,Changmeng Zheng,Xiao-Yong Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Mixture of Debaters (MoD)框架，利用混合专家(MoE)范式在单模型内实现动态自我辩论，解决现有静态架构和计算开销问题。实验表明，相比多智能体系统，准确率更优且延迟降低3.7倍、Token消耗减少87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体辩论框架存在两大关键局限：(1)架构静态，智能体角色与协作模式在构建时固定；(2)需实例化多个模型副本，产生巨大计算开销。这些缺陷限制了框架的效率与灵活性，亟需一种统一框架在单模型内实现高效动态辩论。

Method: 提出Mixture of Debaters (MoD)框架，将MoE范式适配于辩证推理，解决三大挑战：(1)双路由解耦角色分配与流程控制，动态决策辩论/综合时机；(2)动量切换利用局部上下文平滑Token级路由，减少专家切换抖动；(3)统一自我辩论将多样化辩论角色封装为轻量专家模块，消除智能体间通信同时保留行为多样性。

Result: 在多模态基准上进行广泛实验，MoD超越单模型基线与常规多智能体系统，实现更高准确率，同时延迟降低3.7倍，Token消耗减少87%。

Conclusion: MoD框架成功验证了在单一模型内通过MoE机制实现动态自我辩论的可行性与高效性，为多智能体辩论提供了计算友好的替代方案，展示了在保持行为多样性的同时显著提升效率的潜力。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent debate frameworks suffer from two critical limitations: they rely on static architectures where agent roles and coordination patterns are fixed at design time, and they require instantiating multiple model copies, incurring substantial computational overhead. We propose Mixture of Debaters (MoD), a unified framework that enables dynamic self-debate within a single model by leveraging the Mixture-of-Experts paradigm. We address three key challenges in adapting MoE for dialectical reasoning: (1) dual-routing that decouples role allocation from process flow, dynamically determining when to debate versus when to synthesize; (2) momentum switching that smooths token-level routing with local context, reducing expert-switch jitter; and (3) unified self-debate that encapsulates diverse debating personas into lightweight expert modules, eliminating inter-agent communication while preserving behavioral diversity. Extensive experiments on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that MoD outperforms both single-model baselines and conventional multi-agent systems, achieving superior accuracy with 3.7x lower latency and 87% reduction in token consumption.The source code can be accessed at https://github.com/YongLD/MoD.

</details>
