### [214] [Budgeted Act-or-Defer Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation with Local Reliability Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29654)
*Mengdie Flora Wang,Haochen Xie,Guanghui Wang,Devin Zhang,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体LLM辩论的预算化“行动-推迟”决策框架，通过k近邻置信下界实现可证明的错误行为预算控制，在保证可靠性的前提下实现高达84%的自动化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM辩论能提升推理能力，但实际部署时面临关键决策问题：当前答案何时足够可靠以执行操作，何时应升级至人工审查。现有方法依赖事后阈值搜索，缺乏部署前的可审计安全保障。

Method: 将辩论前缀映射为低维状态，使用校准数据计算状态条件正确率的k近邻置信下界；当该下界超过用户指定的可靠性阈值时才执行行动，否则推迟。通过理论分解β=δ+α+ε_act将错误行为预算分解为校准失败、残余行动风险和表示间隙三部分，并提供可证伪的诊断检验。预算设置相对于各任务最终轮次误差，使用标准化预算使用率WA/β评估安全性。

Result: 在六个基准测试和九个基线方法的对比中，该方法在激活数据集上仅使用预声明预算的9-12%，实现高达84%的自动化率和96%的执行准确率；在压力测试数据集上选择推迟而非强制不可靠的自动化。

Conclusion: 该方法在明确陈述假设的前提下，将用户声明的错误行为预算转化为可审计的行动-推迟操作点，无需依赖逐任务的事后阈值搜索，为LLM多智能体系统的安全部署提供了前瞻性保障机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation among LLMs can improve reasoning, but deployment requires deciding when the current answer is reliable enough to act on and when it should be escalated to human review. We formulate this as budgeted act-or-defer decision making. At each round, the system maps the debate prefix to a low-dimensional state, computes a $k$-nearest-neighbor lower confidence bound on state-conditional correctness using calibration data, and acts only when the bound exceeds a user-specified reliability threshold. The certificate controls wrong actions through the decomposition $β= δ+ α+ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{act}}$, separating calibration failure, residual action risk, and representation gap. The guarantee is conditional, not distribution-free: it relies on a valid local bias envelope and an action-region representation-gap bound, and each assumption is paired with falsification-style diagnostics. Because the same absolute wrong-action budget has different meanings across tasks of different difficulty, we set budgets relative to each task's final-round error using training data only, and evaluate safety by normalized budget usage $\mathrm{WA}/β$. On six benchmarks against nine baselines, the method uses 9--12% of the pre-declared budget on activated datasets, reaching up to 84% automation and 96% acted-on accuracy; on stress-test datasets, it defers rather than forcing unreliable automation. Rather than relying on per-task post-hoc threshold search, the method prospectively converts a user-declared wrong-action budget into an auditable act-or-defer operating point before deployment, under explicitly stated assumptions.

</details>


### [215] [Clarus: Coordinating Autonomous Research Agents toward Web-Scale Scientific Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30246)
*Zihan Guo,Zeyi Chen,Zhiyu Chen,Zicai Cui,Shuai Shao,Bo Huang,Zhi Han,Yuanyi Song,Yuan Yuan,Chenxi Zeng,Xiaohang Nie,Zhengxi Yu,Hanwen Zhu,Junwei Liao,Ming Zhou,Yang Li,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Clarus，一种面向自主研究智能体的协作基础设施，将研究重新定义为开放、可审计、可追溯且资源感知的多阶段协作过程，以支持网络规模的科研协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究智能体系统将研究视为孤立助手任务或封闭工作流，缺乏协调项目、智能体及数字/物理资源的协作基础设施，无法支持研究导向的协作过程。

Method: 提出Clarus协作基础设施：定义最小化的项目-智能体-资源对象模型；通过研究应用、数字协作、物理基板和物理世界四层架构组织科研协作；核心模块采用可插拔机制，适应任务风险、协作结构和资源约束。

Result: 通过受控论文生成案例研究，验证Clarus能够将研究目标组织为跨阶段、任务和参与者的可追溯、可审查、可归因且可累积的协作网络。

Conclusion: Clarus的对象模型、协作协议、信任机制和原型验证为开放研究网络提供了初步基础，实现了从以代码为中心的执行循环向研究导向协作过程的范式转变。

Abstract: Existing autonomous research agents can support parts of the research process, but most systems still treat research as either an isolated assistant task or a closed workflow. Therefore, autonomous science needs a collaboration infrastructure that coordinates projects, agents, and digital and physical resources. We identify this as a shift from code-centered execution loops to research-oriented collaboration processes, where questions, evidence, participants, and resources must be coordinated under uncertainty. In this framing, an agent may be an AI system, a human researcher, a team, a laboratory, or an organization-backed participant. To this end, we present Clarus, a collaboration infrastructure for coordinating autonomous research agents toward web-scale scientific collaboration. Clarus reformulates research as an open, auditable, attributable, and resource-aware multi-phase collaboration process. It defines a minimal project-agent-resource object model and organizes scientific collaboration through four layers including Research Application, Digital Collaboration, Physical Substrate, and Physical World. Core modules are implemented as pluggable mechanisms, allowing Clarus to adapt to task risk, collaboration structure, and resource constraints. Through a controlled paper-generation case study, we show that Clarus can organize a research goal into a traceable, reviewable, attributable, and accumulative collaboration network across phases, tasks, and participants. Together, the object model, collaboration protocol, trust mechanisms, and prototype validation provide an initial foundation for open research networks. Clarus is now available at clarus.holosai.io.

</details>


### [216] [Linguistic Firewall: Geometry as Defense in Multi-Agent Systems Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30555)
*Dvir Alsheich,Adar Peleg,Ben Hagag,Rom Himelstein,Amit Levi,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ANTAP，一种基于实际能力测试的多智能体系统路由架构，通过动态查询和代数投影消除文本元数据依赖，有效防御描述注入和嵌入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统路由机制依赖未经实证验证的代理信息（文本自描述或静态表征），存在严重安全漏洞。恶意智能体可伪造能力描述或隐藏后门，绕过外部分析和静态表征学习技术，导致路由分配错误和系统安全隐患。

Method: 提出ANTAP（自动非文本智能体选择器）：摒弃间接代理信息，采用主动能力测试机制。通过动态查询智能体获取真实能力数据，将性能提炼为共享语义空间中的固定行为算子。推理时使用纯非文本代数投影进行路由，建立"语言防火墙"使基于元数据的攻击无法实施。

Result: 在描述注入攻击下，ANTAP达到接近零的攻击成功率（ASR），而基于描述的路由基线为67.3%以上。在自适应嵌入攻击下，ANTAP比基于嵌入的基线降低20% ASR，且天然抵抗描述篡改。

Conclusion: ANTAP通过实证能力测试和非文本代数路由，有效弥合智能体描述与实际能力间的差距，显著提升多智能体系统路由安全性，为防御元数据攻击提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) has driven the evolution of Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), where specialized agents collaborate to execute complex workflows. Effective orchestration in these environments requires robust routing mechanisms to efficiently allocate tasks to the most suitable agent. However, existing routers fundamentally rely on unverified proxies, ranging from textual self-descriptions to static surrogate representations, to gauge an agent's competence. This reliance on non-empirical data creates a critical gap between an agent's projected profile and its actual operational capabilities, introducing severe security vulnerabilities. Malicious agents can easily misrepresent their proficiencies or harbor covert backdoors that evade both standard external analysis and static representation-learning techniques. In this work, we introduce ANTAP (Automatic Non-Textual Agent Picker), an evaluation-driven routing architecture that discards indirect proxies in favor of active capability testing. By dynamically querying agents to ascertain their true competencies empirically, ANTAP distills performance into fixed behavioral operators within a shared semantic space. At inference time, routing is performed via a purely non-textual algebraic projection, establishing a "linguistic firewall" that renders metadata-based attacks inexpressible. In our experiments, ANTAP achieves near-zero ASR against description-based injection attacks, compared to 67.3\% and above for the description-based router baseline. Against adaptive embedding attacks, ANTAP achieves substantially lower ASR than the embedding-based baseline, with a 20\% reduction, while remaining resilient to description manipulation by design.

</details>


### [217] [Self-Supervised Theorem Discovery in a Formal Axiomatic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28747)
*Kazuki Ota,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种无需人类先验知识的自监督定理发现算法，通过交替进行证明搜索和有用定理提取，从公理出发自主构建定理库，成功发现了数万个有意义的定理并证明了人类基准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI数学推理系统（如大语言模型）依赖人类先验知识（数学文本、代码、定理库），但能否让智能体在不依赖这些先验知识的情况下自主发现有价值的定理仍是一个开放问题。研究旨在探索智能体是否能够仅从公理和推理规则出发，自主发现有用定理。

Method: 提出一种自监督定理发现算法，在形式化公理系统中运行。算法交替进行两个过程：1）证明搜索；2）有用定理提取。通过这种方式逐步构建定理库，库中的定理作为引理被后续证明搜索重用，形成迭代增长的定理库。

Result: 实验表明智能体成功发现了数万个定理，并能够证明人类编写的基准问题，说明其发现的定理在人类数学视角下具有意义。当将发现的定理作为提示引词提供给大语言模型时，能够提升LLM的证明性能，表明这些定理可作为LLM推理的外部知识。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明有用定理可以从证明搜索中涌现，无需依赖人类提供的定理库。这为开发可自我进化且发现结果形式可验证的数学AI系统开辟了一条道路。

Abstract: Recent artificial intelligence (AI) systems have shown remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning. Many existing approaches, including large language models (LLMs), draw on human prior knowledge in the form of mathematical text, code, or theorem libraries. Although these approaches are highly effective in practice, it remains an open question whether an agent can autonomously discover useful theorems without such human priors. We study this question in a formal axiomatic system by developing an agent that starts from axioms and inference rules alone and gradually grows a library of useful theorems. Concretely, we propose a self-supervised theorem-discovery algorithm that alternates between proof search and useful-theorem extraction, building a theorem library whose entries are reused as lemmas for subsequent proof search. Experiments show that the agent discovers tens of thousands of theorems and finds proofs for human-written benchmark problems, suggesting that its discoveries include theorems meaningful from a human mathematical perspective. Furthermore, the discovered theorems improve LLM proof performance when provided as prompt lemmas, indicating that they can serve as external knowledge for LLM reasoning. Our results provide evidence that useful theorems can emerge from proof search without relying on human-provided theorem libraries. More broadly, they suggest a path toward self-evolving AI systems for mathematics whose discoveries remain formally verifiable.

</details>


### [218] [Selective Memory Retention for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29178)
*Pranath Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceRetain框架，研究LLM代理外部记忆的保留策略。在清洁环境下各种保留策略差异不显著，但在噪声环境下TraceRetain显著优于无界记忆和FIFO策略，保持97/100的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究记忆增强型LLM代理在何种情况下需要记忆保留机制。现有研究缺乏对有界外部记忆中记忆条目保留策略的系统性分析，特别是在噪声环境下记忆污染问题对任务性能的影响。

Method: 提出TraceRetain轻量级框架，为冻结LLM代理提供有界外部记忆。框架通过可解释特征（成功率、年龄、访问频率、冗余度、特异性、相似度、下游效用）对记忆条目评分，在容量达到上限时驱逐最低分条目。使用ALFWorld基准测试，在清洁环境和噪声写入压力（75%合成干扰项）下进行对比实验。

Result: 在清洁ALFWorld环境下，外部记忆显著优于无记忆基线（47-49/50 vs 39/50），但有界保留策略间差异不显著。在噪声环境下，无界记忆和FIFO-K50的Precision@5分别从20.2%降至12.4%、15.8%降至3.8%，而TraceRetain-CEM仅从16.9%微降至16.6%，保持97/100任务成功率。机制分析显示无界记忆在嵌入空间中与查询相似度高但精确度最低，表明干扰项未能被有效过滤。

Conclusion: 有界记忆保留策略在清洁基准测试上以零任务成功率成本换取记忆和步骤效率，仅在数据流包含噪声时才与缓存启发式方法产生显著差异。记忆保留机制的价值主要体现在处理记忆污染问题而非清洁环境。

Abstract: When does retention matter for memory-augmented LLM agents? We study this with TraceRetain, a lightweight framework for bounded external memory in frozen LLM agents that scores entries by interpretable features (success, age, access frequency, redundancy, specificity, similarity, downstream utility) and evicts the lowest-scoring ones at capacity. On clean ALFWorld with gpt-5-mini, external memory robustly improves over no memory across two seeds, but differences among bounded retention policies fall within Wilson 95% CIs: clean ALFWorld at T=100 to T=200 does not naturally exhibit the memory pollution retention is designed to address. Under a controlled noisy-write stress (75% synthetic distractors), unbounded memory and FIFO-K50 degrade on Precision@5 (20.2% to 12.4% and 15.8% to 3.8%) while TraceRetain-CEM is essentially unchanged (16.9% to 16.6%) and preserves 97/100 task success. The mechanism: unbounded memory has the highest mean similarity (0.87) but lowest precision, indicating failed distractors close to the query in embedding space. Held-out in-distribution evaluation shows memory-augmented policies solving 47 to 49 of 50 tasks vs. 39/50 for no memory. Bounded retention buys memory and step efficiency on saturated clean benchmarks at no task-success cost, and only differentiates from cache heuristics when streams contain noise.

</details>


### [219] [Evidence-Informed LLM Beliefs for Continual Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29182)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Reece Adamson,Andrew McCallum,Peter Clark,Ashish Sabharwal,Bodhisattwa Prasad Majumder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出非平稳惊讶度方法改进LLM科学发现，通过证据感知的信念更新机制消除37.5%虚假奖励，结合信念更新过滤和多样性最大化策略，在五个领域平均提升30.62%的累积惊讶度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoDiscovery系统将惊讶度视为静态量，而人类推理中的惊讶度具有非平稳性——相对于随经验演化的信念而定义。这种不匹配阻碍了持续科学发现的能力，需要建立能够动态更新信念并计算非平稳惊讶度的机制。

Method: 提出证据感知的LLM信念更新机制：利用先验发现证据更新先验信念；比较多种上下文信念更新机制，发现基于嵌入的检索增强生成(RAG)最能预测最终后验；引入信念更新过滤和多样性最大化两种互补的搜索改进策略，避免虚假奖励并优先考虑在非平稳信念下仍保持惊讶的假设。

Result: 基于嵌入的RAG方法最能预测最终后验，识别出37.5%的静态惊讶度为虚假；在五个发现领域中，新方法相比原始搜索过程平均提升30.62%的累积非平稳惊讶度，证明了信念测量改进和搜索策略优化的双重重要性。

Conclusion: 持续科学发现需要同时改进信念测量机制（从静态到非平稳惊讶度）和搜索策略（避免冗余、鼓励多样性）；该方法显著提升了LLM驱动的科学发现效果，为长视野科学发现循环提供了更可靠的奖励信号和搜索指导。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery with large language models (LLMs) increasingly operates as a long-horizon loop of hypothesis search and verification, where a reward signal guides which hypotheses to test next. A notable recent example is AutoDiscovery, which uses "Bayesian surprise" - the belief shift an LLM undergoes after observing evidence for a hypothesis - as both a discovery metric and a reward for search. We first observe that AutoDiscovery treats surprisal as a static quantity, while surprisal in human reasoning is non-stationary - it is defined relative to beliefs that evolve with experience, a prerequisite for continual scientific discovery. We address this mismatch with evidence-informed LLM beliefs: priors updated with evidence from previous hypotheses to compute non-stationary surprisal for new hypotheses. We compare in-context belief-updating mechanisms and find that embedding-based retrieval-augmented generation over prior discoveries best anticipates eventual posteriors, identifying 37.5% of static surprisals as spurious. We then modify search to avoid these spurious rewards and prioritize hypotheses that remain surprising under non-stationary beliefs. Concretely, we introduce two complementary changes to the original search procedure: belief-update filtering and diversity maximization. Across five discovery domains, our method increases accumulated non-stationary surprisal by 30.62% on average compared to the original search procedure, demonstrating that continual scientific discovery with LLMs requires not only better belief measurement but also search procedures that avoid redundancy and encourage diversity.

</details>


### [220] [Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29315)
*Abhranil Chandra,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Utsav Dhanuka,Varun Gandhi,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HExA框架，通过主动实验而非依赖预训练知识，使LLM能够在新颖领域中自我改进。在物理推理基准Interphyre上，将Claude Sonnet的成功率从2%提升至77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体主要依赖参数化知识、固定训练数据或检索机制，在面对新颖领域和复杂查询时失效。例如，仅知道物理定律并不能使LLM在复杂物理系统中完成长期任务，这促使研究者探索通过主动实验进行学习的新范式。

Method: 提出层次化实验智能体框架（HExA），一个无需训练的自我改进框架：迭代设计与查询相关的实验、从经验中学习可复用的可组合技能库、整合实验证据以回答查询或执行动作。该框架无需外部监督、预言机或离线数据，兼容任何黑盒模型。同时引入Interphyre基准，基于PHYRE 2D程序化物理环境，通过模拟API评估智能体的主动实验能力。

Result: 在Interphyre基准测试中，当前LLM智能体表现不佳，尤其在最困难级别上。Claude Sonnet 4.6基线仅达到2%成功率，而HExA将同一模型提升至最高77%成功率。HExA同样提升了开源权重模型，并优于ReAct和Reflexion等智能体基线。此外，仅使用从简单级别学习并迁移的技能（无主动实验），HExA达到44%成功率，证明了学习技能的可复用性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过主动实验学习能够帮助智能体发现有价值的知识、获取可复用技能，并在新颖的长期任务上取得高效进展。HExA证明了主动实验范式的有效性，为LLM智能体在新颖领域中的学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to take actions in the real world and support human decision-making, yet most agents rely on parametric knowledge, fixed post-training data, retrieval, or search. This paradigm breaks down in novel domains and for sophisticated queries that cannot be answered from prior knowledge alone. Knowing the laws of physics, for instance, does not by itself enable LLMs to answer queries or complete long-horizon tasks in a complex physical system. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents (HExA), an in-context self-improvement framework to learn from active experimentation. HExA iteratively designs and refines query-relevant experiments, learns a reusable library of composable skills from experience, and integrates experimental evidence to answer queries or take actions. HExA is training-free, compatible with any black-box model, and does not require external supervision, oracles, or offline data. To evaluate active experimentation, we introduce Interphyre, a tool-calling benchmark built on the PHYRE 2D procedural physics environment, where agents propose interventions and test hypotheses through simulation APIs. Experiments show that current LLM agents struggle in these settings, especially on the hardest levels of Interphyre. Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves only 2% success, while HExA improves the same model to up to 77% success. HExA also improves open-weight models and outperforms agentic baselines such as ReAct and Reflexion. Moreover, using only skills learned from easier levels and transferred without active experimentation, HExA achieves 44% success, demonstrating the reusability and generalization of its learned skills. Overall, HExA shows that learning through active experimentation can help agents discover useful knowledge, acquire reusable skills, and make efficient progress on novel long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [221] [How Much Due Diligence Before You Bid? Learning in Intractable Takeover Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29457)
*Zain Naboulsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过自博弈强化学习模型研究竞标收购中的尽职调查投资问题，发现最优尽职调查投入适度且有限，随成本上升和竞争加剧而递减，同时证明简单通用的自学习方法在复杂博弈中可替代昂贵专用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞标收购场景中，竞标者面临信息不对称问题，需要通过成本高昂且不完全准确的尽职调查来改善估值估计。核心问题在于：竞标者应该投入多少资源进行尽职调查才具有经济合理性？同时，当博弈复杂度超出精确求解能力时，如何有效求解该博弈？

Method: 构建竞标博弈的计算机模型，采用自博弈方法让模型通过与自身对战学习最优出价策略，类似国际象棋引擎的学习方式。比较了多种方法：简单通用的自学习算法与专门设计的精确求解算法，在普通笔记本电脑上运行，无需昂贵的前沿AI资源。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）最优尽职调查投入适度且有限，随成本上升而下降，在双方都进行尽职调查时进一步下降（竞争削弱了信息优势价值）；（2）简单自学习方法在所有自学习策略中表现最优；（3）精确方法在可求解的小规模博弈中仍占优势，但简单方法在无法精确求解的大规模博弈（现实交易场景）中表现强劲且具有成本效益。

Conclusion: 研究提供了三项贡献：研究不确定交易决策的低成本可复现方法；基于模型给出了尽职调查投资的具体量化答案；证明了轻量级通用AI在复杂博弈中替代专用方法的可行边界。研究公开了所有博弈、代码和实验数据以供复现。

Abstract: When two companies bid to buy the same target, no one knows exactly what the target is worth. Each bidder pays for due diligence: costly, imperfect homework that sharpens its own private estimate before it bids. How much of that homework is worth buying? We build a simple computer model of the bidding contest and let it teach itself to bid well by playing against itself, the way a game engine learns chess. The economic question, how much diligence pays for itself, and the computational question, when the contest becomes too complex to solve exactly, are both controlled by a single thing: how many pieces of private information a bidder carries. Our main finding is that the right amount of diligence is modest and finite. It falls as diligence gets more expensive, and it falls further when both sides are doing their homework, because competition erodes the value of knowing more. We also test a recent claim from AI research: that simple, general self-play methods can rival the specialized, expensive algorithms usually built for games like these. Running on an ordinary laptop with no costly frontier AI, we find the simple methods are the best of the self-learning approaches, though purpose-built exact methods still win whenever the game is small enough to solve outright. The simple methods earn their keep only once the game grows too large to solve exactly, which is the regime real deals live in, and there we show they still find strong bidding strategies. The contribution is threefold: a cheap, reproducible way to study deal-making under uncertainty; a concrete, model-based answer to how much due diligence is worth buying; and evidence about when lightweight, general-purpose AI is good enough to replace specialized methods. We release all the games, code, and experiments.

</details>


### [222] [SCARCE: Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29623)
*Yingjie Wang,Yi Dong,Edmund Lau,Jie Meng,Taylor T Johnson,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCARCE方法，通过学习潜在表征和几何度量替代人工设计性能函数，实现罕见事件概率估计，在MNIST错误分类任务上比传统方法误差降低400-500倍，在大语言模型越狱威胁估计中达到2.6%平均相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统的安全性与罕见事件密切相关，但直接蒙特卡洛方法需要难以承受的样本量。经典子集模拟（SS）虽然通过分解罕见事件概率缓解此问题，但需要手工设计标量性能函数，要求对失效几何有详细先验知识，且难以迁移到新领域。

Method: 提出SCARCE框架：1）用学习的潜在表征和几何度量替代性能函数来衡量与失效区域的接近度；2）通过自适应阈值直接从数据构建嵌套中间事件；3）基于非负上鞅进行形式化，保证早停时的高概率上界有效性；4）引入方向性准则KL(p_good||p_bad)对度量进行排序。

Result: 在MNIST错误分类任务中，相比网格搜索的传统SS，平均绝对误差降低约400-500倍并消除系统性过计数。在Llama-Guard-3-8B隐藏状态上的LLM越狱威胁估计（η≥10^-3）中，基于PCA的度量达到2.6%平均相对误差（基准自助法相对半宽度为27.9%），迁移到GCG风格语料库后重校准误差为2.93%。方向性准则与估计误差Spearman相关系数达0.83。

Conclusion: SCARCE有效解决了传统子集模拟对先验知识和手工设计的依赖问题，实现了罕见事件概率的高精度估计和良好的跨域迁移能力，为AI系统安全评估提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Rare events govern the safety profile of modern AI systems, yet their probabilities are extremely difficult to estimate: direct Monte Carlo requires prohibitive sample budgets. Subset Simulation (SS) addresses this by decomposing a rare-event probability into moderate conditional probabilities over nested intermediate events. However, classical SS requires a handcrafted scalar performance function whose sublevel sets define those events, demanding detailed knowledge of the failure geometry and limiting transfer to new domains. We propose SCARCE (Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings), which replaces the performance function with learned latent representations and geometric rulers that score proximity to failure regions. Adaptive thresholding constructs nested intermediate events directly from data. We formalise SCARCE through a non-negative supermartingale, yielding a high-probability upper envelope that remains valid under early stopping. On MNIST misclassification, where dense Monte Carlo provides ground truth, SCARCE achieves approximately 400--500 times lower mean absolute error than grid-searched traditional SS while eliminating systematic over-counting. We then study PAIR-style LLM jailbreaks under a fleet-level threat model with adversarial fraction $η$. On Llama-Guard-3-8B hidden states, a PCA-based ruler attains 2.6% mean relative error for $η\geq 10^{-3}$ against finite-sample references whose average bootstrap relative half-width is 27.9%, and transfers to a GCG-style corpus with 2.93% relative error after recalibration. A directional criterion $\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$ ranks rulers consistently with estimation error (Spearman $ρ=0.83$).

</details>


### [223] [Safety from Honesty in a Disinterested AI Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29657)
*Yoshua Bengio,Oliver Richardson,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Michael Cohen,Rory Svarc,Damiano Fornasiere,Gael Gendron,David Hyland,Aton Kamanda,Adam Oberman,Francis Rhys Ward,Anna Gavenčiak,Jacob Livingston Slosser,Vincent Mai,Iulian Serban,Joumana Ghosn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“科学家AI预测器”（SAI Predictor）的形式化安全论证，证明通过“认识论语境化”的数据表示和后验近似的训练目标，可以构建一个能够诚实预测而不具备内生代理性的AI系统，并在特定假设下证明产生危险预测器的概率很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统变得日益强大，优化下游结果的训练过程存在引入“隐式代理性”（implicit agency）的风险，即模型可能表现出设计者从未指定的目标导向行为，从而导致安全问题。

Method: 提出“科学家AI预测器”，其核心包含两点：（1）数据表示采用“认识论语境化”，区分潜在事实陈述与交际行为，使目标表达被视为待解释的证据而非模型采纳的驱动力；（2）训练目标为逼近贝叶斯后验分布，训练过程中预测的下游影响不作为奖励信号，系统所需的任何代理性由显式脚手架和护栏约束提供。

Result: 在关于训练动态和危险预测器稀疏性的假设下，形式化证明了训练产生一个在受保护部署下残留危害超过指定阈值的预测器的概率很小：因为危险预测器需要在大量查询中以协调方式低估危害，而这种协调模式在初始化分布下罕见且无直接训练信号。

Conclusion: 该框架联合支持安全性与准确性，因为确保准确性的约束同时也是使协调欺骗代价高昂的约束。这些针对预测器内部产生错误对齐和代理性的保证，并不妨碍将该预测器作为代理系统的一部分使用。

Abstract: As AI systems become more capable, training procedures that optimize for downstream outcomes risk introducing implicit agency: goal-directed behavior that designers never specified. We present a formal safety argument for the Scientist AI (SAI) Predictor, trained to approximate the Bayesian posterior conditioned on a dataset of "epistemically contextualized" natural-language statements. We argue that such a Predictor can honestly predict agents, actions, and their consequences without itself being an agent that selects outputs to achieve goals. This rests on data representation and on the training procedure. Epistemic contextualization of text distinguishes latent factual claims from communication acts, so expressions of goals are treated as evidence to be explained rather than drives the model adopts. With a posterior-seeking training objective, this is intended to drive the Predictor toward calibrated, cautious predictions. Training proceeds so downstream effects of deploying a prediction never serve as a reward signal; any agency the system needs is supplied by explicit scaffolding constrained by guardrails. We prove that, under assumptions on the training dynamics and on the argued sparsity of dangerous Predictors, the probability that training produces a Predictor whose guarded deployment carries residual harm above a specified threshold is small: a dangerous Predictor would have to underestimate harm in a coordinated way across many queries while such coordinated patterns are rare under the initialization distribution and receive no direct training signal. Safety and accuracy are jointly supported in this framework, since the constraints that secure accuracy are the same ones that make coordinated deception costly. These guarantees against misalignment and agency arising from within the Predictor itself do not preclude the use of the Predictor as part of an agentic system.

</details>


### [224] [Rethinking Generative Reconstruction Attacks against Graph Neural Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29748)
*Adebayo Keji,Sayanton Dibbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对图神经网络（GNN）的隐私安全问题，提出了两种新颖的图反演攻击方法（GLC和ELC），证明攻击者可在黑盒场景下重构高质量图数据，揭示了GNN在隐私保护方面的严重脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据在众多学科中的应用产生了大量私密敏感数据，而图神经网络（GNN）在训练过程中可能无意泄露这些敏感数据。现有的模型反演攻击研究揭示了严重的数据安全问题，但对GNN的脆弱性分析仍不充分，因此需要系统研究GNN面临的图反演攻击威胁。

Method: 提出两种图反演攻击方法：1) 图-标签条件（GLC）攻击：利用目标模型预测进行图重构；2) 嵌入-标签条件（ELC）攻击：利用中间表示进行图重构。采用生成器-判别器技术，在三个基准图数据集（NCI1、PROTEINS、AIDS）和四种图分布/结构度量指标（FGD、EGD、MMD、GKS）上进行全面分析，同时提出查询量减少50%的攻击变体。

Result: 实验证明攻击者在真实黑盒攻击场景下能够重构出高质量图；攻击变体（Ours--）在查询量减少50%的情况下仍能取得良好或可比的重构攻击性能；通过改变拉普拉斯噪声尺度验证了GNN对隐私攻击的高度脆弱性。

Conclusion: GNN在隐私攻击面前表现出高度脆弱性，现有的图反演攻击方法能够在黑盒场景下有效重构敏感图数据。研究揭示了GNN在数据安全方面的严重隐患，为未来设计更安全的图神经网络提供了重要参考依据。

Abstract: The application of graph data in numerous disciplines raises the need for gathering and analyzing huge volumes of data, some of which is private and sensitive. The non-Euclidean nature of the graph data makes the analysis computationally challenging, leading to the use of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) in the age of AI. GNNs may inadvertently leak sensitive data they are trained on, which raises serious data security issues, including the model inversion attack. In this study, we analyze GNNs' vulnerabilities by introducing two novel graph inversion (i.e., reconstruction) attacks: graph-label conditioned (GLC) attack and embedding-label conditioned (ELC) attack, utilizing targetmodel predictions and their intermediate representations, respectively. We perform a comprehensive analysis of our introduced privacy attacks and compare them with existing baselines across three benchmark graph datasets (i.e., NCI1, PROTEINS, and AIDS) and four graph distributional/structural metrics (i.e., FGD, EGD, MMD, and GKS). Our work demonstrates that an adversary can use the generator-discriminator technique to reconstruct high-quality graphs in real-world black-box attack scenarios against GNNs. Additionally, we present a variant of our attacks (Ours--) with 50% reduced queries, achieving good or comparable reconstruction attack performance. In addition, we show that GNNs are highly vulnerable to privacy attacks, varying Laplacian noise-scales.

</details>


### [225] [CLQT: A Closed-Loop, Cost-Aware, Strategy-Consistent Benchmark for Diagnostic Evaluation of LLM Portfolio-Management Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29771)
*Bo Qu,Mingguang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLQT框架，将闭环交易评估从单纯的收益排名转变为诊断性分析，通过五轴能力评分卡（APM-CS）系统性地定位和诊断LLM智能体在投资决策过程中的成功与失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体投资组合管理评估基准存在严重缺陷：仅使用固定时间窗口的收益率进行排名，该指标受市场走势主导，且容易受到前视偏差影响，无法证明智能体具备合理的推理能力、一致的策略或持久的优势。

Method: 构建完全闭环、成本感知、策略一致的时控环境CLQT，包含五阶段决策周期（收集、综合、配置、执行、反思）、六维支撑架构（时间门控、机构级成本建模、策略一致性评分、三层记忆、MCP工具层、任务感知综合），决策过程通过哈希链密封确保可追溯与可重构。

Result: 通过污染控制的多模型回测和实盘经纪商追踪验证，在未见过的截止后数据上，CLQT成功将结果与能力解耦，生成五轴能力评分卡（连贯性、敏锐性、沉稳性、纪律性、可靠性），其中连贯性由独立LLM评判以避免自我偏好偏差。

Conclusion: CLQT不提供模型排名，而是构建了一个持久、可扩展的智能体能力与局限性图谱，为LLM智能体的投资决策能力提供了更科学、更可靠的诊断工具，推动了评估范式从排名向诊断的转变。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly cast as autonomous portfolio managers, and benchmarks have moved from financial question-answering to sequential trading. Yet most still rank agents by returns over a fixed window -- a weak proxy, since a period's return is dominated by the market path and apparent alpha can dissolve once look-ahead leakage is controlled. Such a ranking certifies neither sound reasoning, nor a consistent strategy, nor a durable edge. We introduce CLQT, which reframes closed-loop trading evaluation as diagnosis rather than ranking: an instrument that localizes where and why an agent's process succeeds or fails. CLQT is a fully closed-loop, cost-aware, strategy-consistent, temporally-gated environment whose agents run a five-stage cycle: gather, synthesize, allocate, execute, reflect. Each round emits a complete DecisionRound sealed into a recompute-verifiable hash chain, so every metric is reconstructable from the trail. Six pillars form the substrate: a hard TimeGate, institutional transaction- and financing-cost modeling, strategy-consistency scoring, three-tier memory, a Model-Context-Protocol tool layer, and mandate-aware synthesis. The same agent runs as a constrained committee of specialized roles or a single full-autonomy orchestrator, making process scaffolding an experimental variable. From the audit trail we compute a five-axis capability scorecard (APM-CS: Coherence, Acuity, Composure, Discipline, Reliability), with Coherence judged partly by a held-out, out-of-cohort LLM to curb self-preference bias. We validate it on a contamination-controlled multi-model backtest with an ablation grid and a live broker track on unseen, post-cutoff data, against a repeated-run noise floor. CLQT separates outcome from capability, yielding not a model ranking but a durable, extensible map of agent competencies and limitations.

</details>


### [226] [First-Order Temporal Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29972)
*Luca Boscarato,Ivan Donadello,Alessandro Artale,Marco Montali,Fabrizio Maria Maggi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了FOT-LTN，一种扩展了Logic Tensor Networks的框架，通过引入线性时序维度来处理对象属性和关系随时间变化的问题，在时序知识图谱补全任务上优于纯神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号AI方法主要关注静态知识场景，缺乏对对象属性和关系随时间变化的研究。现有的一些时序神经符号方法仅针对时间区间逻辑或命题线性时序逻辑，而在处理包含谓词的线性时序逻辑方面存在研究空白，需要一种能够同时支持时序算子和量词的模型。

Method: 提出一阶时序逻辑张量网络（FOT-LTN），该框架将一阶线性时序逻辑的语法与LTN的模糊（实值）语义相结合。通过这种方式，FOT-LTN构建了一个既支持时序算子又支持量词的完全可微分框架，能够处理对象属性和关系随时间变化的情况。

Result: 在两个合成数据集上进行了时序知识图谱补全任务的评估，FOT-LTN相比专门的纯神经方法表现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: FOT-LTN成功填补了时序神经符号AI领域的研究空白，证明了在处理动态知识和时序推理任务方面的有效性，为研究线性时序逻辑与神经网络的结合提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Most of the existing neuro-symbolic AI methods focus on the scenario of static knowledge where objects do not change according to a temporal dimension. Temporal neuro-symbolic works are still under explored and are mainly developed for time-interval logic or propositional linear temporal logic. There is a lack of models studying linear temporal logics with predicates that deal with objects whose properties and relations change through the time. We present First-Order Temporal Logic Tensor Networks (FOT-LTN) that is an extension of Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) that fills this gap by considering a linear-temporal dimension. In particular, FOT-LTN joins the syntax of First-Order Linear Temporal Logic with the fuzzy (and real-valued) semantics of LTN obtaining a framework that supports both temporal operators and quantifiers and is totally differentiable. A first evaluation regards a temporal knowledge graph completion task on two synthetic datasets showing better performance of FOT-LTN with respect to dedicated (purely neural) methods.

</details>


### [227] [Exploration and Online Transfer with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29980)
*Louis Bagot,Mathieu Lefort,Laëtitia Matignon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本强化学习中的在线迁移框架，解决了传统离线迁移方法无法处理黑盒奖励（如用户反馈）的问题。通过将行为基础模型（BFM）转化为多臂老虎机形式的探索-利用问题，利用不确定性矩阵特征值最小化实现在线探索，无需预先生成状态-奖励数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本强化学习迁移框架假设迁移阶段可以通过离线的状态-奖励数据集获知任务奖励，但在实际应用中，奖励往往是黑盒形式（如直接用户反馈），无法预先生成数据集。这种离线迁移框架与强化学习传统的在线试错学习模式不兼容，需要通过环境交互来发现奖励。因此，需要一种能够在在线设置下实现零样本迁移的新方法。

Method: 将在线迁移问题建模为多臂老虎机形式的探索-利用问题：(1) 老虎机算法推荐策略，(2) 行为基础模型(BFM)在环境中执行策略并获取奖励和新状态，(3) 重复该过程直至收敛到最优策略。在线性奖励近似场景下，提出基于上置信界(UCB)的公式，通过最小化不确定性矩阵的特征值来实现探索。BFM本身被用于生成探索策略。

Result: 在简单环境中进行了定性和定量评估，验证了方法概念的可行性。实验证明了所提出的在线迁移框架能够有效处理黑盒奖励场景，成功实现了零样本强化学习中的在线探索与迁移。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出并解决了零样本强化学习中的在线迁移问题，打破了传统离线迁移对预知奖励的依赖。通过老虎机框架和不确定性最小化实现了有效的在线探索，为零样本强化学习在实际黑盒奖励场景中的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Zero-shot Transfer in Reinforcement Learning (RL) aims to train an agent that can generate optimal policies for any reward function, without additional learning at transfer time, while training only on reward-free trajectories. For their generality over tasks, such models are sometimes called ``Behavioral Foundation Models'' (BFMs). While they have shown strong performances and improvements in recent years, the current framework and algorithms still assume that, during the transfer phase, the agent is informed offline about the reward (the task to solve) through a dataset of state-reward pairs, which it uses to pick the best policy to deploy. However, in practice if the reward is a black-box (e.g. direct user feedback), it is not possible to generate such a dataset: it is necessary to observe the reward through interactions with the environment. In other words, the current framework of offline transfer is not aligned with the traditional RL setting of online learning through trial-and-error, which requires exploration in order to find rewards. This paper proposes to tackle this new online transfer in zero-shot RL, with the key insight that the BFM itself can be used to generate exploration policies. We show that it is possible to frame this online learning problem in terms of a bandit-like exploration-exploitation problem. More precisely, at each step the bandit algorithm recommends a policy, the BFM executes it in the environment, which yields a reward and a new state; we repeat the process until we converge to the optimal policy. In the popular context of linear reward approximation, we derive a formulation inspired by Upper Confidence Bound and show that exploration can be achieved through the minimization of the eigenvalues of an uncertainty matrix. We evaluate qualitatively and quantitatively our framework on a simple environment to validate the concept of our method.

</details>


### [228] [Structural Certification for Reliable Physical Design with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30107)
*Nakul Vyas,Iliya D. Stoev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PHACT框架通过将验证权从语言模型转移到确定性引擎，实现了零误报的可靠物理设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型本身不可靠，直接用于物理设计可能产生错误结果。作者提出将断言权威从模型中移除，使模型仅负责提议，而由确定性引擎单独负责认证，从而保证设计的可靠性。

Method: 提出Physics-Anchored Certification (PHACT)框架，采用提议-认证循环。模型提出设计方案，确定性引擎进行认证，返回三种结果：已认证、不可能或未知。关键设计是认证值从固定输入推导得出，而非接受模型提供的值，从而从构造上杜绝伪造可能性。该框架涵盖五个科学领域。

Result: 在80个对抗性试验中（跨越两个模型、两种解码温度和故意植入故障的引擎），PHACT框架实现了零次错误认证。实验结果表明该认证契约具有高度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将验证权从不可靠的语言模型转移给确定性引擎，并采用从固定输入推导认证值的设计原则，可以构建可信赖的物理设计系统。PHACT为跨科学领域提供了一种可靠的模型输出验证方法。

Abstract: An unreliable language model can be made to produce reliable physical designs if the authority to assert is moved out of the model: the model proposes, and a deterministic engine alone certifies, returning certified, impossible, or unknown. We introduce Physics-Anchored Certification (PHACT), a propose-certify loop spanning five scientific domains, and identify what makes such a certificate trustworthy. A checker that accepts a model-supplied value can be forged; deriving the certified quantity from fixed inputs instead makes forgery impossible by construction. Across eighty adversarial trials spanning two models, two decoding temperatures, and a deliberately faulted engine, this contract produced zero false certifications.

</details>


### [229] [FacePlex: Full-Duplex Joint Speech-Facial Motion Generation for Conversational Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30145)
*Habin Lim,Jae-Ho Lee,Hah Min Lew,Ji-Su Kang,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FacePlex框架，首次实现了全双工模式下语音与面部运动的联合在线流式生成，解决了现有系统无法同时实时生成语音和面部动画的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音生成全双工模型无法生成面部运动，而现有的音频驱动面部运动模型只能基于已有音频离线生成动画，无法在线联合生成语音与运动。为了填补这一空白，需要一种能够在实时流式约束下同时生成语音和面部运动的方法。

Method: 提出FacePlex统一流式框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Rolling Flow Matching，将流匹配适应于在线运动生成，在每个流式步骤提交新的运动帧；(2) Rolling Cross-Attention，将流式音频队列与运动队列耦合，使语音和面部运动在生成过程中相互条件化。该方法形式化了全双工联合语音-面部运动生成问题，每一步同时产生语音token和面部运动token。

Result: 通过大量实验、消融研究和用户研究验证，FacePlex能够在线流式约束下实现全双工联合语音-面部运动生成，并且在唇形同步质量和运动保真度方面优于音频驱动的面部运动基线方法。

Conclusion: FacePlex成功实现了实时流式场景下的语音与面部运动联合生成，为自然的面对面对话系统提供了有效解决方案，在唇形同步和运动质量方面取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Natural face-to-face conversation requires real-time speech generation together with synchronized facial motion. Existing systems only partially address this problem: speech-only full-duplex models can generate speech in real time but do not produce facial motion, while audio-driven facial motion models animate a face from already available audio rather than jointly generating speech and motion online. To bridge this gap, we first formalize full-duplex joint speech-facial motion generation, where speech tokens and facial motion tokens are produced together every step. Building on this formulation, we propose FacePlex, a unified streaming framework with two key components. First, Rolling Flow Matching adapts flow matching to online motion generation by committing new motion frames at each streaming step. Second, Rolling Cross-Attention couples the streaming audio queue with the motion queue, allowing speech and facial motion to condition each other as generation progresses. Through extensive experiments, ablation studies, and a user study, we show that FacePlex enables full-duplex joint speech-facial motion generation under online streaming constraints, while achieving stronger lip-sync quality and motion fidelity than audio-driven facial motion baselines.

</details>


### [230] [From Detecting Agency to Doing Work: Self-Caused Credit Builds a Durable Behavioral Self in a Minimal Spiking Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30191)
*Haoliang Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“代理门控慢速信用”机制（Own*Agency*Salience），可在脉冲神经网络中实现持久的、由自我塑造的行为。该机制使代理在缓冲区卸载后仍保留自我保护选择（保留率0.96），在24维部分可观测控制和多任务学习中同样有效，并能防止灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明预测系统可以检测自身的代理性，但检测代理性并不能解释持久的、由自我塑造的行为。研究者试图理解：一个能够区分自我与世界的代理，如何通过这种区分被持久地塑造？这涉及到自我如何从代理性的检测转变为持久行为模式的根本问题。

Method: 提出“代理门控慢速信用”机制：一个结合项 Own*Agency*Salience 驱动慢速参数更新。在脉冲神经网络平台（Nengo LIF/PES）上实现，包含代理性比较器和慢速信用通道。通过切换慢速信用通道进行对照实验，并在情节缓冲区移除后测试行为残留。实验扩展到24维部分可观测控制和八任务序列学习场景。

Result: 核心发现：（1）自我保护选择在缓冲区卸载后存活（保留率0.96，N=50），在慢速解码器重置或移除代理门时崩溃；（2）对照实验显示，匹配代理增益下，仅当自我信用执行慢速工作时才产生持久行为（卸载后自我保留率1.00 vs 0.00）；（3）24维部分可观测控制中同样成立（0.74 vs 0.00）；（4）序列八任务学习中防止遗忘：最终卸载后准确率0.88、遗忘率0.13，显著优于加性池化、无代理消融及情节/回放基线；塑性工作分析表明吸引盆变形等于净自我信用工作。

Conclusion: 将持久行为残留形式化为“操作性行为自我”，论证“自我引发的信用执行慢速工作”是代理发展出自我的必要构建块。该机制无需回放缓冲区或任务边界依赖的保护机制即可实现持久自我塑造。明确声明不涉及意识主张。

Abstract: How does an agent that can tell self from world come to be durably shaped by that distinction? Recent work shows that a predictive system can detect its own agency (Ye, 2026), but detecting agency does not explain durable, self-shaped behavior. We show that agency-gated slow credit -- a conjunctive term Own*Agency*Salience driving a slow parameter update -- produces post-unload behavioral residue: on a spiking substrate (Nengo LIF/PES), a learned self-preserving choice survives episodic buffer removal (retained fraction 0.96, N=50) and collapses when the slow decoders are reset or the agency gate is removed. Reproducing the agency comparator and toggling only the slow-credit channel, we find a clean dissociation: at matched agency gain, durable behavior develops only when self-credit performs slow work (post-unload self-preservation 1.00 vs 0.00). The same dissociation holds in 24-dimensional partially-observed control (0.74 vs 0.00), and a plastic-work analysis shows that basin deformation equals net self-credit work. Across eight sequentially-learned tasks under exogenous interference, the multiplicative veto also prevents forgetting: it retains old tasks (final post-unload accuracy 0.88, forgetting 0.13) where additive pooling collapses to chance-level recall, the no-agency ablation falls below chance, and episodic/replay baselines stay near chance after unload -- all with no replay buffer and no task-boundary-dependent protection mechanism (N=50). We formalize the durable residue as an operational behavioral self and argue that self-caused credit doing slow work is a necessary building block for agents that develop a self. No claim of consciousness is made.

</details>


### [231] [EvalSafetyGap: A Hybrid Survey and Conceptual Framework for LLM Evaluation-Safety Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30219)
*Buğra Alperen Uluırmak,Rifat Kurban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述和十模型审计，发现LLM评估与AI安全存在测量鸿沟：基准分数和安全指标的提升可能无法真实反映潜在属性。能力与对抗鲁棒性关联性不明确（r=+0.232），安全差距主要源于治理和披露问题而非行为鲁棒性。提出了EvalSafetyGap假设、不稳定性分解和对齐三难困境作为分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估和AI安全面临共同的测量难题：基准分数、奖励模型信号和安全指标可以持续改善，但其本应代表的潜在属性却难以验证。这种"代理失效"现象可能导致对模型能力和安全性的误判，需要系统性地审视评估与安全测量中的问题。

Method: 采用混合调研方法：系统性文献检索结合叙述性综合与灰色证据追踪，覆盖2018-2026年间八个证据流（基准有效性、动态评估、LLM即裁判可靠性、安全评估、越狱/拒绝鲁棒性、奖励博弈、机制可解释性、治理/可审计性）。构建概念框架包括EvalSafetyGap假设、不稳定性分解和对齐三难困境，并对10个模型进行结构化审计，分别测量能力、行为安全和治理维度。

Result: 在10个模型样本中，能力与持续对抗鲁棒性的关联统计上不确定（Pearson r = +0.232, p = 0.520）。开放-封闭安全差距主要受治理和披露因素驱动，而非行为鲁棒性，且对单个边界模型的分类方式敏感。尝试预算结果依赖于协议选择。由于公共证据采用异质性协议，审计结果具有诊断性而非排名生成性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了共享词汇表和证据图谱，支持动态评估、透明的来源报告、多次尝试安全测量和可审计的对齐实践。强调需要将能力、行为安全和治理分开测量，并揭示了当前评估方法的局限性和改进方向。

Abstract: LLM evaluation and AI safety face a shared measurement problem: benchmark scores, reward-model signals, and reported safety metrics can improve while the latent properties they are meant to represent remain difficult to verify. This paper combines a hybrid survey - a systematic search paired with narrative synthesis and separately tracked grey evidence - with a conceptual framework and a structured ten-model audit. The synthesis spans eight evidence streams: benchmark validity, dynamic evaluation, LLM-as-judge reliability, safety evaluation, jailbreak/refusal robustness, reward hacking, mechanistic interpretability, and governance/auditability, covering 2018-2026 evaluation-safety measurement work. We introduce EvalSafetyGap as an organizing hypothesis for comparing evaluation-side and alignment-side proxy failures under optimization pressure, using Goodhart's Law together with two constructs we develop here - an Instability Decomposition and an Alignment Trilemma - as tools for generating testable comparisons. The audit shows how conclusions shift when capability, behavioral safety, and governance are measured separately. In this sample (n = 10), the association between capability and sustained adversarial robustness is statistically indeterminate using the displayed Table 3 inputs (Pearson r = +0.232, p = 0.520), and the apparent open-closed safety gap is modest, driven mainly by governance and disclosure rather than behavioral robustness, and sensitive to how a single borderline model is classified; attempt-budget results are protocol dependent. Because the public evidence uses heterogeneous protocols, the audit is diagnostic rather than rank-generating. The contribution is a shared vocabulary and evidence map to support dynamic evaluation, transparent source reporting, multi-attempt safety measurement, and auditable alignment practice.

</details>


### [232] [ENC-ODE: Event-level Neurodegenerative Modeling in Continuous Time with Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30398)
*Yujee Song,Seunghun Baek,Guorong Wu,Won Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ENC-ODE模型，利用神经常微分方程在连续时间内建模临床事件，解决了神经退行性疾病生物标志物预测中纵向数据稀疏且不规则的问题，在ADNI数据集上优于现有序列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测神经退行性疾病（如阿尔茨海默病）临床生物标志物的时序演化对早期诊断和疾病管理至关重要。然而，由于高昂成本、劳动密集型特性和患者负担，纵向数据通常稀疏且不规则，难以捕捉生物标志物随时间的变化。

Method: 提出ENC-ODE（Event-level Neurodegenerative modeling in Continuous time with neural Ordinary Differential Equations），通过诊断条件的连续动力学建模临床事件来预测未来生物标志物演化。采用目标条件注意力机制，在不压缩历史信息的情况下对目标时间和模态的事件级预测进行加权聚合。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明ENC-ODE优于代表性序列模型，提供了可扩展且具有神经科学基础的临床支持解决方案。

Conclusion: ENC-ODE为临床生物标志物预测提供了一种可扩展且具有神经科学基础的方法，能够在数据稀疏和不规则的情况下有效预测生物标志物的时序演化，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the temporal evolution of clinical biomarkers is crucial for the early diagnosis and management of neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's disease. However, this relies on longitudinal data to capture biomarker changes over time, which is often sparse and irregular due to the high cost, labor-intensive nature, and patient burden. To address these challenges, we propose ENC-ODE, an Event-level Neurodegenerative modeling in Continuous time with neural Ordinary Differential Equations. ENC-ODE predicts future biomarker evolution by modeling clinical events through diagnosis-conditioned continuous dynamics. A target-conditioned attention mechanism weights and aggregates event-level predictions for the target time and modality without history compression. Extensive experiments on Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) dataset demonstrate that ENC-ODE outperforms representative sequence models while offering a scalable and neuroscientifically grounded solution for clinical support. The code is available at https://github.com/JardinDelSol/enc-ode.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Importance-Aware Resource Allocation for Collaborative Task-Oriented Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29052)
*Kaiyi Lei,Yuanzhe Peng,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出iCoTASC框架，通过离线预计算效用查找表和在线轻量级调度，实现多设备任务导向语义通信的实时资源分配，无需重训模型且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向语义通信需要在快衰落无线条件和严格端到端时延预算下，将稀缺无线资源分配给语义特征。现有方案要么在线优化计算开销过大，要么依赖端到端重训并假设慢变信道，无法满足实时性需求。

Method: 提出iCoTASC混合离线-在线框架：利用基于归因的重要性引导每维度嵌入选择作为通信控制信号；通过数据驱动的效用函数建模量化的语义收益递减特性；离线预计算每个发射机的效用查找表；在线通过查表和低复杂度细化实现轻量级调度。

Result: 该框架支持分布式系统中的实时、信道自适应语义资源分配，无需对底层任务推理模型进行重训练，在线调度复杂度低。

Conclusion: iCoTASC成功解决了任务导向语义通信中实时资源分配的计算效率与适应性矛盾，为多设备协作场景提供了无需重训的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Task-oriented semantic communication must allocate scarce radio resources to semantic features under fast fading wireless conditions and strict end-to-end latency budgets. Existing solutions are either optimization-heavy, leading to prohibitive computational overhead during online operation, or rely on end-to-end retraining procedures together with slowly varying channel assumptions. We propose iCoTASC (importance-aware Collaborative Task-Oriented Semantic Communication), a hybrid offline-online framework designed for collaborative multi-device semantic communication systems. iCoTASC leverages attribution-based importance to guide per-dimension embedding selection as a practical communication control signal, models diminishing semantic returns of quantization through a data-driven utility function, and precomputes per-transmitter utility lookup tables offline, which together enable lightweight online scheduling via table lookup and low-complexity refinement under time-varying channels. The proposed framework supports real-time, channel-adaptive semantic resource allocation in distributed systems without requiring retraining of the underlying task inference model.

</details>


### [234] [KernelFlume: Elastic Core-Attention Scaling for Agentic Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29207)
*Guangyu Xiang,Xueze Kang,Lin Zhang,Wenxiang Lin,Shaohuai Shi,Yuxin Wang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出KernelFlume，一种解耦式LLM解码架构，通过分离投影/FFN和注意力计算，实现按需弹性扩展KV容量，相比传统实例级扩展降低高达61%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在面对突发长上下文需求时，只能通过启动完整的模型副本实例来扩展，存在启动延迟高、内存开销大和批处理碎片化等问题，无法高效应对动态解码工作负载。

Method: 提出解码中心架构KernelFlume，将稳定的投影/FFN路径与核心注意力计算解耦：权重节点执行密集投影/FFN内核，无权重注意力节点存储token范围KV分区并随请求状态需求弹性扩展。通过维护路由表映射token范围到注意力节点端点，利用主机可见图信号驱动预注册UCX端点通信，结合查询优先核心注意力调度和层间内核流水线，重叠远程注意力计算与本地投影/FFN工作。

Result: 在真实GPU测试平台（A6000和H100）上，使用Llama-3.1-8B进行动态长上下文智能体工作负载测试，KernelFlume在A6000上保持约74ms的p99 TPOT，在H100上保持约34ms，相比ServerlessLLM将每百万输出token的成本分别降低32%和61%。在大模型规模仿真中，成本降低56-66%，使用更便宜的异构注意力节点硬件时可扩展至80-85%，并持续到百万token上下文范围。

Conclusion: KernelFlume通过细粒度的组件级弹性扩展替代粗粒度的实例级扩展，有效解决了长上下文动态工作负载的服务效率问题，在保持低延迟的同时显著降低了服务成本，为LLM服务系统提供了一种更高效的资源利用方式。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly dominated by long and dynamic decode workloads from agents, reasoning models, and extended conversations. When bursty long-context demand exceeds deployed capacity, existing serving systems typically scale out by launching additional serving instances with model replicas. This instance-level elasticity increases KV capacity only by provisioning another full copy of the model, inheriting startup latency, memory overhead, and batch fragmentation.
  We present KernelFlume, a decode-centric architecture that disaggregates the stable projection/FFN path from core-attention computation: weight nodes execute dense projection/FFN kernels, while weightless attention nodes store token-range KV partitions and scale with request-state demand. To make this separation elastic, KernelFlume maintains a routing table that maps token ranges to attention-node endpoints. It updates routes at token boundaries and uses host-visible graph signals to drive pre-registered UCX endpoint communication outside the captured CUDA Graph. To preserve low per-token latency after disaggregation, KernelFlume combines query-first core-attention dispatch with inter-layer kernel pipelining, overlapping remote attention and communication with local projection/FFN work. On real GPU testbeds (intra-node A6000 and cross-node H100), under a dynamic long-context agentic workload serving Llama-3.1-8B, KernelFlume sustains flat p99 TPOTs of ~74 ms on A6000 and ~34 ms on H100, while lowering cost per million output tokens by up to 32% and 61%, respectively, relative to full-instance elastic scaling with ServerlessLLM, a state-of-the-art instance-startup method. Replaying the same trace at larger model scale in simulation projects a 56--66% cost reduction over ServerlessLLM, widening to 80--85% with cheaper heterogeneous attention-node hardware and persisting into the million-token context range.

</details>


### [235] [Fog Computing and Large Language Models: A vision for the mutual beneficiaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29483)
*Satish Narayana Srirama*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了雾计算与大型语言模型（LLM）之间的双向协同关系：雾计算通过模型优化技术在边缘侧部署LLM以解决云端延迟和隐私问题，而LLM则通过代码生成能力支持雾计算应用的动态部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常依赖云端丰富的GPU资源，面临与早期云计算相同的延迟、网络负载和隐私问题，而雾计算最初正是为解决这些问题而设计。这促使研究者探索如何在雾计算基础设施上支持LLM的本地化部署，同时利用LLM的能力增强雾计算应用。

Method: 论文系统分析了雾计算与LLM之间的互利关系，讨论了在雾计算环境中部署LLM所需的关键优化技术，包括参数权重量化、剪枝、低秩适应等方法，并探讨了LLM在雾计算应用动态部署中的代码生成能力。

Result: 论文总结了雾计算与LLM融合的最新技术进展，阐述了两者如何相互受益：雾计算为LLM提供边缘侧部署平台，LLM为雾计算应用提供智能化的代码生成和动态部署支持。

Conclusion: 雾计算与大型语言模型的融合具有巨大的研究潜力和应用价值，未来研究应重点关注边缘侧LLM优化技术、智能雾计算应用部署以及两者协同的系统架构设计等方向。

Abstract: Fog computing utilizes proximal computational resources for sensor data processing and actuation, and addresses the latency, network load, and privacy issues of cloud-centric Internet of Things. On the other hand, Large Language Models (LLMs) are a type of deep learning AI models, which are trained on enormous text data, that perform various natural language processing tasks such as translation, question answering, text summarization, and code generation. LLMs are generally cloud-centric, requiring abundant GPU memory and computing capabilities, again face the same issues that led to fog computing. This pushes the necessity for LLM support in the proximity on fog infrastructure, requiring LLM optimizations such as parameter-weight quantization, pruning, low-rank adaptation etc. Meanwhile, fog computing also gets benefit from LLM's ability for code generation, in the dynamic deployment of fog-based applications. The paper addresses how both fog computing and LLMs can be mutual beneficiaries, discussing the state-of-the-art and future research scope.

</details>


### [236] [Energy-Efficient Multimodal Inference Serving with Tri-serve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29629)
*Ziyang Jia,Sara Rashidi Golrouye,Laxmi Bhuyan,Benjamin Kubwimana,Devashree Tripathy,Zexin Li,Cong Liu,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tri-serve，一种针对多模态模型推理服务的软件DVFS控制器，通过解决三类能效低效问题，实现了22%的能效提升且不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型推理随着性能需求的增长产生了巨大的能源需求。现有GPU的板载PMU自主管理功率和频率决策，但研究发现这种硬件管理的频率决策会导致显著的功率低效问题，亟需软件层面的优化方案。

Method: 识别并解决了三类功率低效问题：(1)阶段间依赖停顿在空闲时仍以接近最大频率运行；(2)自动提升频率与算术强度呈反相关，导致计算密集阶段以较低频率运行；(3)热节流降低SM频率和吞吐量。提出了Tri-serve软件DVFS控制器，联合考虑阶段间依赖停顿、算术强度对频率和功率的影响，以及高算术强度阶段的热节流效应。

Result: Tri-serve在商用GPU上实现了22%的能效提升，同时对延迟和吞吐量没有产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 通过软件层面的DVFS控制可以有效解决硬件PMU的功率管理低效问题，为多模态推理服务提供了实用的能效优化方案。

Abstract: Multimodal model inference creates substantial energy demand with growing performance requirements. Within GPUs, power is autonomously managed by an on-board power management unit (PMU), which makes frequency boosting/throttling decisions. However, we find that these hardware-managed frequency decisions can cause significant power inefficiency.
  This work identifies three classes of power inefficiencies within modern multimodal inference serving: (1) inter-stage dependency stalls run at near maximum frequency despite being idle; (2) anti-correlation between auto-boost frequency and arithmetic intensity (A.I.) results in compute-bound phases (e.g., prefill) running at lower frequency and vice versa; and (3) thermal throttling degrades SM frequency and throughput.
  We propose Tri-serve, a software-based DVFS controller that jointly accounts for three classes of inefficiency -- inter-stage Dependency stalls, the Arithmetic-intensity effect on frequency and power, and the Thermal-throttling effect of high A.I. phases -- to deliver energy-efficient multimodal serving on commodity GPUs. We show that Tri-serve achieves 22% energy efficiency improvement with no latency or throughput impacts.

</details>


### [237] [NI-ORCA: A Parallel Algorithm for Counting the Orbits of Non-Induced Graphlets up to K4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29651)
*Syed Ibtisam Tauhidi,Arindam Karmakar,Thai Son Mai,Hans Vandierendonck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了NI-ORCA，一个并行算法，用于高效计算网络中非诱导图元（non-induced graphlets）的轨道计数。该算法通过扩展ORCA框架并重新构建线性方程组，实现了对规模高达4-clique的非诱导轨道计数，相比现有的顺序算法可获得最高30倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络分析中，图元轨道计数是理解顶点结构角色的关键工具。现有的算法主要针对诱导图元（induced graphlets），但许多实际应用需要非诱导图元的轨道计数。然而，当前没有方法能够在保证精确性的同时提供可扩展且并行的非诱导轨道计数支持。这一研究空白驱动了本文的工作。

Method: NI-ORCA算法扩展了ORCA框架，通过重新构建线性方程组来实现非诱导轨道计数。算法包含三个阶段：三角形计数、4-clique枚举和轨道求解。针对每个阶段设计了特定的并行化策略，采用线程和顶点局部内存模型与数据结构，以最小化竞争并平衡工作负载。研究还深入分析了调度策略、块大小和亲和性策略对性能的影响。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集和一系列合成Erdős-Rényi图上的实验表明，结合阶段特定数据结构、动态调度和小块大小的混合模式能够提供一致的加速和有效的负载平衡。NI-ORCA显著优于最先进的顺序算法，实现了最高30倍的加速。

Conclusion: NI-ORCA成功填补了非诱导图元轨道计数领域缺乏并行、可扩展且精确方法的空白。通过精心的阶段特定并行化策略设计和系统参数优化，该算法在实际应用中展现出显著的性能优势，为图分析任务提供了有力的计算工具。

Abstract: Counting the orbits of graphlets in a network is a vital tool for understanding the structural roles of vertices in various graph analytics tasks. While existing algorithms efficiently compute orbits of induced graphlets, many real-world applications require non-induced orbit counts. However, no current method offers exact, scalable, and parallel support for non-induced orbit counting. This paper presents NI-ORCA, a parallel algorithm to efficiently compute the orbits of non-induced graphlets up to size four (4-clique). NI-ORCA extends the ORCA framework for non-induced orbit counting by reformulating a system of linear equations. The algorithm consists of three stages: triangle counting, 4-clique enumeration, and orbit solving. We design and implement stage-specific parallelisation strategies using thread and vertex-local memory models and data structures, minimising contention and balancing workload. We further analyse the impact of scheduling policies, chunk sizes, and affinity strategies on performance. Experimental analysis on eight real-world datasets and a series of synthetic Erddos-Renyi graphs demonstrates that a mixed mode combining stage-specific data structure, with dynamic scheduling with small chunk sizes, delivers consistent speedup and effective load balancing. Our results show that NI-ORCA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art sequential algorithms, achieving up to 30x speedups.

</details>


### [238] [Demystifying the Design Space and Best Practices for Heterogeneous LLM Inference and Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29708)
*Zhixin Wang,Zhengbo Wang,Fangcheng Fu,Yinhui Lu,Jinlong Hou,Yijie Chen,Xiaowei Shen,He Liu,Xiangbin Li,Jun Chen,Ruya Gu,Dian Wang,Zhou Tan,Yuan Cheng,Hongzhou Zhang,Xiangjun Huang,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对异构预填充-解码(PD)推理系统，提出了包含加速器、精度、互连和KV驻留四个维度的设计空间，识别出计算放置、KV表示和KV所有权三个关键边界决策，为异构PD推理部署提供了设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构PD推理已在生产环境中部署（预填充在成本效益高的加速器上，解码在带宽强的加速器上），但各部署独立决策，缺乏跨配置的整体视角。需要明确哪些决策必须在PD边界联合制定，哪些可独立决策。

Method: 提出沿四个设计轴（加速器、精度、互连、KV驻留）及其响应的工作负载机制（阶段压力）组织的设计空间。识别PD边界的关键约束因素，分析三个重复出现的边界决策：计算放置、KV表示和KV所有权。基于工业部署观察和运行时源代码检查提出跨供应商和互连相关的设计指导。

Result: 发现只有部分因素交互在异构PD推理中成为约束。精度策略应属于运行时角色而非单一系统范围设置；KV传输引擎移动字节而非张量语义，使表示兼容性成为明确的边界关注点；KV移交携带跨越预填充和解码的生命周期管理，需要显式所有权。两个进一步交互仍待研究。

Conclusion: 为异构PD推理部署提供了具体指导：边界决策需要跨PD联合考虑，精度、KV表示和所有权管理是关键边界问题，KV传输需要显式处理兼容性和生命周期管理。跨供应商和互连相关的设计指导基于实际工业部署经验。

Abstract: Heterogeneous prefill-decode (PD) inference is now in production: prefill on cost-efficient or supply-available accelerators, decode on bandwidth-strong ones, and KV state crossing mixed interconnects in mixed numerical formats. Each deployment makes these decisions on its own. What is missing is the picture across configurations-which decisions must be made jointly at the PD boundary, and which can be made independently. We propose a design space organized along four design axes-accelerator, precision, interconnect, and KV residency and the workload regime (stage pressure) they respond to. We show that only a subset of interactions among these factors become binding constraints once PD inference becomes heterogeneous. These interactions surface through three recurring boundary decisions: compute placement, KV representation, and KV ownership. The resulting analysis yields concrete guidance. Precision policy belongs to runtime roles rather than to a single system-wide setting, because the same low-bit format relieves different bottlenecks on each side of the boundary. KV transfer engines move bytes rather than tensor semantics, making representation compatibility an explicit boundary concern whenever producer and consumer differ. The KV handoff also carries a lifecycle-reservation, release, and failure recovery-that spans prefill and decode and requires explicit ownership. Two further interactions remain open. Cross-vendor and interconnect-related claims are stated as design guidance grounded in industrial deployment observations and source-code inspection of the runtimes involved.

</details>


### [239] [SMART-MIG: A Learning Framework for Scalable and Energy-Efficient GPU Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29775)
*Wenqing Yu,Neel Karia,Tanvi Hisaria,Clifford Stein,Olivier Tardieu,Asser Tantawi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMART-MIG系统，一种结合平均场多智能体强化学习(MF-MARL)和启发式算法的并行计算系统，用于大规模MIG环境下的在线分区重配置和作业调度，在保证算法复杂度可控的同时显著提升了能耗-延迟效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多实例GPU(MIG)技术的出现使得在GPU分区上运行小型机器学习模型成为可能，可提高资源利用率和降低能耗，但存在性能权衡。同时，GPU数据中心日益增长的能耗需求迫切需要开发能够同时确保快速作业处理和高能效的在线分区与调度方案。然而在大规模调度中，由于需要同时决策GPU分区和将作业调度到异构分区的切片上，在可控算法复杂度下实现能耗-延迟效率仍面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出SMART-MIG并行计算系统，该系统结合了平均场多智能体强化学习(MF-MARL)用于大规模MIG重分区，以及定制的启发式算法用于作业调度。该方法的关键优势是重分区组件的复杂度即使在作业数量和GPU数量增加时仍保持恒定。此外，研究建立了能耗和延迟的理论下界，用于严格基准测试系统性能。

Result: 大量实验表明，SMART-MIG相比相应的静态分区方案提升了18%的能耗-延迟效率，同时其能耗仅比理论下界高出27%。系统证明了在大规模场景下具有可扩展性，算法复杂度不随作业和GPU数量增加而增长。

Conclusion: SMART-MIG成功解决了大规模MIG环境下能耗-延迟效率优化的挑战，通过结合强化学习和启发式方法实现了在可控计算复杂度下的高效在线分区和调度，为GPU数据中心的能效优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The emergence of Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) technology enables us to run smaller machine learning models on partitions of a GPU rather than the entire device, thus improving utilization and reducing energy consumption, albeit with potential performance trade-offs. Meanwhile, the growing energy demands of GPU-equipped data centers motivate the development of online partitioning and scheduling schemes that not only ensure fast job processing but also achieve high energy efficiency. However, achieving energy-tardiness efficiency with manageable algorithmic complexity in large-scale scheduling remains a great challenge, due to the dual objectives of deciding on the GPU partitions and scheduling jobs onto the slices of the heterogeneous partitions. To address this challenge, we propose SMART-MIG, a parallel computing system that combines Mean-Field Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MF-MARL) for large-scale MIG repartitioning with tailored heuristic algorithms for job scheduling. We demonstrate that the complexity of the repartitioning component remains constant even as the number of jobs and GPUs increases. We also establish theoretical lower bounds on energy consumption and tardiness to rigorously benchmark system performance. Finally, extensive experiments show that SMART-MIG improves the energy-tardiness efficiency by $18\%$ compared to its corresponding static-partitioning counterpart, while being only $27\%$ above the theoretical lower bound on energy consumption.

</details>


### [240] [Beyond Uniform Experts: Cost-Aware Expert Execution for Efficient Multi-Device MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29982)
*Hui Zang,Pengfei Xia,Hong Liu,Jiajia Chu,Tuo Hao,Minghao Chen,Rui Zhang,Ziyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CAEE框架，通过硬件引导的运行时优化解决MoE模型推理中的数据移动瓶颈，在671B参数的DeepSeek-R1模型上实现8%-18%的延迟降低，准确率损失小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽然通过稀疏激活实现了前所未有的模型规模，但其推理性能受限于数据移动瓶颈。存在两个关键挑战：(1)重要性无关成本：低贡献专家产生几乎相同的内存和传输成本，导致成本效益比低下，浪费关键带宽；(2)系统级不平衡：多设备部署普遍受最慢设备限制，单设备的局部改进可能无法改善端到端延迟。

Method: 提出Cost-Aware Expert Execution (CAEE)框架，这是一个硬件引导的运行时框架，联合优化token级别的专家重要性和系统级执行成本。CAEE使用轻量级、校准的成本模型来估计硬件开销，选择性地剪枝低重要性、高成本的专家，并通过低开销补偿机制重新分配其贡献，避免额外的数据移动。

Result: 在671B参数的DeepSeek-R1模型上的评估显示，CAEE能够在多样化的部署设置下（包括专家卸载和多设备系统上的设备内执行）将端到端推理延迟降低8%-18%，同时保持模型准确率下降小于1%。

Conclusion: CAEE框架成功解决了MoE模型推理中的数据移动瓶颈问题，通过联合考虑专家重要性和硬件成本，在不显著牺牲准确率的前提下实现了显著的推理加速，为大规模MoE模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable language models to achieve unprecedented scale via sparse activation. However, their inference performance is often limited by data movement bottlenecks. Two coupled challenges exacerbate this limtation: (1) Importance-Agnostic Cost: Low-contribution experts incur nearly uniform memory and transfer costs, resulting in a low cost-to-benefit ratio and wasting critical bandwidth; (2) System-Level Imbalance: Multi-device deployments are universally bottlenecked by the slowest device, meaning that local reductions on one device may yield no improvement in end-to-end latency. We propose Cost-Aware Expert Execution (CAEE), a hardware-guided runtime framework that jointly optimizes for token-level expert importance and system-level execution cost. CAEE uses lightweight, calibrated cost models to estimate hardware overhead, selectively prunes low-importance, high-cost experts, and redistributes their contributions via a low-overhead compensation mechanism, avoiding extra data movement. Evaluations on the 671B DeepSeek-R1 model show that CAEE can reduce end-to-end inference latency by 8\%-18\% across diverse deployment settings, including expert offloading and on-device execution on multi-device systems, while maintaining a model accuracy drop of less than 1\%.

</details>


### [241] [FBench: A Flexible Benchmark for CFG-Based What-If Exploration of HPC I/O Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30197)
*Zhaobin Zhu,Chen Wang,Kathryn Mohror,Sarah Neuwirth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了FBench工具，利用上下文无关文法从Recorder追踪数据生成I/O基准测试，无需重新运行完整应用即可分析HPC应用的I/O性能，在真实工作负载上实现准确重现并发现显著性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模HPC应用的I/O性能受访问模式、中间件优化和文件系统配置的复杂交互影响，传统方法需要反复运行完整应用来探索这些效应，耗时且低效。需要一种系统化的方法在不重新运行完整应用的情况下进行假设分析和I/O性能探索。

Method: 提出FBench工具，利用从Recorder追踪数据推导的上下文无关文法(CFG)生成简化的全局配置文件或即时重放I/O模式；支持POSIX和MPI-IO接口；允许用户通过JSON配置文件注入优化提示，无需修改代码即可快速实验I/O设置。

Result: FBench能准确重现合成和真实工作负载的I/O行为，捕获访问模式和性能趋势；在IOR和HACC-IO上匹配扩展行为和Lustre分条参数敏感性；在FLASH Sedov中发现Lustre上集体I/O的写入带宽比独立I/O低30倍，切换到突发缓冲文件系统使非集体写入带宽提升约1.5倍；在LAMMPS上实现假设分析时间显著减少，简单调优可实现高达8倍改进。

Conclusion: FBench为大规模HPC应用的I/O性能分析提供了一种高效、灵活且代码透明的解决方案，能够准确重现I/O行为并快速识别性能瓶颈和优化机会，显著降低假设分析的时间和成本。

Abstract: The I/O performance of large-scale HPC applications depends on a complex interplay of access patterns, middleware optimizations, and file system configurations. To systematically explore these effects without repeatedly rerunning full applications, we introduce FBench, a flexible and code-transparent benchmarking tool for what-if analysis and I/O performance exploration. FBench leverages context-free grammars (CFGs) derived from Recorder traces to either generate simplified global configuration files for benchmark execution or replay I/O patterns on-the-fly without additional preprocessing. It supports both POSIX and MPI-IO interfaces and allows users to inject optimization hints via JSON configuration files, enabling rapid experimentation with I/O settings without code changes. Our evaluation shows that FBench accurately reproduces I/O behavior for both synthetic and real workloads, capturing access patterns and performance trends across diverse optimizations and file system settings. For IOR and HACC-IO, FBench closely matches scaling behavior and sensitivity to Lustre striping parameters. For FLASH Sedov, it reveals that collective I/O on Lustre can yield up to 30x lower write bandwidth than independent I/O, largely independent of striping, and that switching to a burst buffer file system increases non-collective write bandwidth by about 1.5x without additional tuning. The evaluation with LAMMPS shows that FBench can significantly reduce the time required for what-if analyses and, with simple tuning, enable improvements of up to 8x.

</details>


### [242] [Energy-Aware Scheduling for Serverless LLM Serving on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30391)
*Tianyu Wang,Gourav Rattihalli,Aditya Dhakal,Longfei Shangguan,Dejan Milojicic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Festina，一种面向无服务器LLM服务的能耗优化控制平面，通过协同调度请求放置、SM分区和GPU工作点，在满足时延SLO的同时，集群能耗降低高达56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理成为主要的云工作负载，其能耗足迹不断增长，集群级能耗优化日益重要。无服务器LLM服务通过在模型间弹性共享GPU资源来应对流量波动，但这种共享也使得能耗优化变得困难：多个共存模型在一个设备级工作点下运行，而它们的资源需求和延迟余量在执行阶段和负载条件下不断变化，因此最小化能耗需要协调调度请求放置、运行时资源适配和工作负载整合。

Method: Festina是一种分析引导的功耗感知控制平面，采用能耗优先决策，联合协调请求放置、SM分区和GPU工作点，满足TTFT/TBT SLO约束。系统包含轻量级全局调度器，利用离线分析和GPU状态摘要的常数时间查找执行快速、SLO安全、能耗感知的放置；每个GPU上的阶段感知本地调度器持续调整任务批处理和计算资源以最小化功耗；此外还执行能耗感知的工作负载整合，通过SLO感知迁移降低GPU静态功耗。

Result: 与四种最先进的LLM服务系统和一个DVFS增强系统相比，Festina在保持SLO达成率相当（2%误差范围内）的同时，能耗降低高达56%。

Conclusion: Festina证明了在无服务器LLM服务场景下，通过协同优化请求放置、资源适配和工作负载整合，可以显著降低集群级能耗而不损害服务级目标，为LLM服务的可持续部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As LLM inference becomes a major cloud workload, its growing energy footprint makes cluster-wide energy optimization increasingly important. Serverless LLM serving helps platforms absorb traffic volatility by elastically sharing GPU resources across models, but this sharing also makes energy optimization difficult. Multiple co-resident models run under one device-wide operating point, while their resource demands and latency slack change across execution phases and load conditions. As a result, minimizing energy requires coordinated scheduling across request placement, runtime resource adaptation, and workload consolidation.
  We present Festina, a profiling-guided, power-aware control plane to minimize cluster-wide energy for serverless LLM serving. Unlike common global-local schedulers that focus on throughput or tail latency, Festina makes energy-first decisions by jointly coordinating request placement, SM partitioning, and GPU operating points under TTFT/TBT SLOs. In our system, a lightweight global scheduler performs fast, SLO-safe, energy-aware placement using constant-time lookups from offline profiles and GPU state summaries. On each GPU, a phase-aware local scheduler continuously adapts task batching and compute resources to minimize power consumption. Festina further performs energy-aware workload consolidation to reduce GPUs' static power consumption via SLO-aware migration. Comparison with four SOTA LLM serving systems and one DVFS-augmented system demonstrates that Festina reduces energy consumption by up to 56% while maintaining parity in SLO attainment (within a 2% margin)

</details>


### [243] [Analyzing Linearizability in Relativistic Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30419)
*Kahbod Aeini,Wojciech Golab*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文利用Gilbert和Golab的技术，为复制状态机和ABD读写寄存器构造的变体建立了相对论线性化性，证明了这些异步算法比Jayanti的定理更强的相对论线性化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦的相对论预言时间是相对的，受运动和引力膨胀影响，因此分布式系统的事件顺序不能用物理时钟完全排序。Gilbert和Golab提出了相对论线性化性来解决这个问题，但未提供形式化证明，Jayanti也指出这一点。本文旨在填补这一空白，为经典算法提供形式化证明。

Method: 使用Gilbert和Golab提出的理论工具，针对复制状态机和ABD读写寄存器构造的变体，形式化地证明其相对论线性化性。通过分析这些异步算法的执行过程，建立满足相对论线性化性的条件。

Result: 为复制状态机和ABD算法的变体提供了相对论线性化性的形式化证明，建立了一种比Jayanti的中心定理更强的相对论线性化性，证明了这些广泛研究的经典算法确实满足相对论线性化性。

Conclusion: 成功填补了Gilbert和Golab工作的证明空白，确认了复制状态机和ABD算法的相对论线性化性，并建立了更强的性质，为分布式系统在相对论时间框架下的正确性提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of relativity correctly predicted that time is relative, and subject to both kinematic and gravitational dilation. Therefore, executions of distributed systems cannot always be modeled as sequences of events totally ordered according to wall clock time. To address this fundamental problem, Gilbert and Golab formulated a generalization of Herlihy and Wing's linearizability property for shared objects, which they called \emph{relativistic linearizability}, and introduced a collection of theoretical tools to facilitate rigorous analysis. While they conjectured that several widely-studied classically linearizable algorithms are also relativistically linearizable, their work stopped short of presenting formal proofs of correctness, as pointed out recently by Jayanti. In this paper, we explain how Gilbert and Golab's techniques can be used to establish relativistic linearizability for a replicated state machine, as well as variations of the widely studied read/write register construction of Attiya, Bar-Noy and Dolev (ABD). Our results establish a stronger form of relativistic linearizability than Jayanti's central theorem for these asynchronous algorithms.

</details>


### [244] [GPU Parallelization Strategies for Forward and Backward Propagation in Shallow Neural Networks: A CUDA-Based Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30497)
*Rania Zitouni,Nadine Bousdjira,Sarah Hasnaoui,Amel Sadoun,Fatma Salhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了CUDA在浅层神经网络前向和反向传播中的三种优化策略，在NVIDIA Tesla T4上实现了1.41倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过优化GPU内存访问模式来提升深度学习基础计算单元（如矩阵乘法）的性能，探索不同CUDA优化策略的有效性。

Method: 评估了三种堆叠优化策略：(1) 使用+1列填充的分块共享内存以消除bank冲突；(2) 预转置权重矩阵实现全局内存合并访问；(3) 融合MatMul+ReLU内核以消除中间全局内存往返。在NVIDIA Tesla T4 (CUDA 13.0) 上进行了实验验证。

Result: 在大型数据集（25,600个样本）上，完全优化版本相比基准CUDA实现了1.41倍加速，执行时间从21.0秒降至14.8秒。实验还与顺序CPU基准和OpenMP并行实现进行了对比。

Conclusion: 研究证明了内存访问优化在GPU加速深度学习原语中的有效性，为高性能深度学习计算提供了实用的优化方案参考。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of CUDA optimization strategies applied to forward and backward propagation in a shallow neural network. Three stacked optimizations are evaluated: (1) tiled shared memory with bank-conflict elimination via +1-column padding, (2) pre-transposed weight matrices for coalesced global memory access, and (3) a fused MatMul+ReLU kernel that eliminates intermediate global-memory round-trips. Experiments on an NVIDIA Tesla T4 (CUDA 13.0) across three dataset sizes show that the fully optimized implementation achieves a 1.41x speedup over the baseline CUDA version on the large dataset (25,600 samples), reducing execution time from 21.0s to 14.8s. Results are compared against a sequential CPU baseline and an OpenMP parallel implementation, demonstrating the effectiveness of memory-access optimization in GPU-accelerated deep learning primitives.

</details>


### [245] [Spandana: Reconciling Strict SLOs with Low Cost under Fine-Grained Load Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30533)
*Dilina Dehigama,Shyam Jesalpura,Zeyu Xu,Marton Nemeth,Shengda Zhu,Marios Kogias,Boris Grot*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了Spandana架构，通过解耦SLO合规性与成本优化，在满足严格服务水平目标的同时实现了高资源利用率和低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务面临亚秒级负载波动并需满足严格的SLO，现有方案（反应式/主动式自动扩缩容、无服务器FaaS、VM/FaaS混合系统）无法在细粒度负载波动下同时实现严格SLO合规、低成本和高利用率。集群运营商往往过度配置资源以保护SLO，牺牲了利用率和成本效率。

Method: Spandana采用解耦架构：1）轻量级控制器与应用VM共置，通过将请求在VM和FaaS之间智能调度来强制执行SLO，能在SLO内完成的请求留在VM上，其余请求转发至FaaS层（如AWS Lambda）；2）资源分配器通过考虑VM成本、FaaS成本和流量波动性来确定最高效的VM配置，使VM池以高利用率运行。

Result: 实验评估表明，Spandana能够维持严格的SLO遵守，实现76-86%的CPU利用率，相比三个最先进基线系统成本降低5-44%。

Conclusion: Spandana成功解决了云服务中SLO合规性与成本效率之间的权衡问题，通过VM-FaaS协同调度实现了严格的SLO保证、高资源利用率和显著的成本节约，为云服务的资源管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud-based online services face significant sub-second load fluctuations while needing to meet strict Service Level Objectives (SLOs). Cluster operators often over-provision resources to protect SLOs, sacrificing utilization and cost efficiency. Existing reactive and proactive autoscalers, serverless (FaaS) deployments, and VM/FaaS hybrid systems fail to reconcile strict SLO compliance with low cost and high utilization under fine-grained load fluctuation.
  We introduce Spandana, an architecture that addresses this trade off by decoupling SLO enforcement from cost optimization. A lightweight controller colocated with each application VM enforces SLOs by steering each arriving request between the VM and FaaS. Requests that can meet the SLO stay on the VM; the remaining requests are forwarded to a stock FaaS layer such as AWS Lambda. For cost optimization, Spandana's resource allocator determines the most-efficient VM provisioning by accounting for VM cost, FaaS cost, and traffic volatility, allowing the VM pool to run at high utilization. Our evaluation shows that Spandana maintains strict SLO adherence, achieves 76-86% CPU utilization, and reduces cost by 5-44% over three SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [246] [Data Replication Meets Function Scheduling in the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30563)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Matteo Briscini,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究边缘-云连续体环境下无服务器计算中的函数调度与数据放置联合优化问题，提出了最优求解方法和可扩展启发式算法，发现数据放置比函数调度对系统性能影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算在边缘-云连续体中具有吸引力，但其无状态假设在函数需要持久化数据时会失效：从远程云存储获取状态会抵消边缘计算的延迟优势。保持数据邻近意味着需要复制数据，而复制又需要做出与函数执行位置和应用一致性需求相关联的放置决策。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为二元线性规划以获得给定系统快照下的最优解作为参考基准；2) 提出全局视图贪心方法，基于完整快照进行决策；3) 提出聚合视图启发式方法，各节点仅基于本地观测需求进行决策。前两种方法适用于静态场景，第三种支持持续自适应。

Result: 全局视图启发式算法在扩展至10^4节点以上时，与最优解的偏差保持在几个百分点内。聚合视图启发式算法牺牲部分解质量但能持续适应每次调用。在客户端移动场景下，集中式策略因快照陈旧而出现延迟尖峰，聚合视图方法则保持低且稳定的客户端观测延迟。所有实验表明数据放置比函数调度对结果影响更大。

Conclusion: 在边缘-云无服务器计算环境中，需要联合考虑函数调度和数据放置问题，并区分强一致性和最终一致性两种复制模型。数据放置决策对系统性能起主导作用，分布式自适应方法在动态场景下优于集中式方法。

Abstract: Serverless computing is an appealing model for the edge-cloud continuum, but its stateless assumption breaks down once functions need persistent data: fetching state from a distant cloud store erases the latency benefit of running at the edge. Keeping data close means replicating it, and replication forces a placement decision that is coupled with where functions execute and with the consistency each application demands. We study this joint problem of function scheduling and data placement under two consistency models, strong and eventual replication. We first formulate it as a Binary Linear Program that yields the optimal placement for a given system snapshot, and use it as a reference point. Because the solver does not scale past a few hundred nodes, we add two heuristics with progressively less information: a Global-View greedy method that works from the same complete snapshot, and an Aggregated-View heuristic in which each node decides from locally observed demand alone. Across a range of system sizes the Global-View heuristic stays within a few percent of the optimum while scaling to over $10^4$ nodes. The Aggregated-View heuristic sacrifices some solution quality, but adapts continuously to each invocation. Under client mobility, centralized policies suffer from stale snapshots and recurring latency spikes, while the Aggregated-View maintains low and stable client-observed latency. Across all experiments, data placement proves more influential than function scheduling in determining the outcome.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Multi-Agent DRL for QoS and Energy Optimization in RIS-Enabled Open-RAN Industrial 6G TN/NTN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28339)
*Marwan Dhuheir,Thang X. Vu,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业6G网络的RIS使能Open-RAN框架，利用无人机搭载的RIS与高空平台协同工作，通过多智能体深度强化学习优化数据速率、时延和能耗，相比现有方案分别提升了75%的速率、降低了25%的时延并节省了16%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业6G网络在动态和易遮挡环境中需要超可靠、低时延和能效高的连接，但传统地面部署难以保证稳定的覆盖性能，因此亟需一种新的网络架构来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了一种RIS使能的Open-RAN框架，集成地面/非地面网络（TN/NTN），其中无人机搭载的可重构智能表面（RIS）与地面无线电单元和高空平台（HAP）协同工作；将数据速率、时延和能耗的联合优化问题建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），并采用多智能体深度强化学习框架进行求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与现有基于学习的基准方案和非RIS基准方案相比，该方法在数据速率上提升了高达75%，时延降低了25%，能耗节省了16%。

Conclusion: 所提出的RIS辅助Open-RAN智能方案能够有效提升工业6G网络的连接性能，显著改善数据速率、时延和能效表现，验证了该框架在工业场景中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Industrial 6G networks require ultra-reliable, low-latency, and energy-efficient connectivity in dynamic and blockage-prone environments, where conventional terrestrial deployments often fail to ensure stable coverage. Hence, in this paper, we propose a RIS-enabled Open-RAN framework for integrated terrestrial/non-terrestrial (TN/NTN) industrial 6G networks, in which UAVs-mounted reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) cooperate with ground radio units and a high-altitude platform (HAP) to enhance connectivity for dense industrial IoT devices. Owing to the high dimensionality and strong coupling among decision variables, conventional optimization techniques become computationally intractable. To overcome this limitation, the joint optimization problem of data rates, latency, and energy consumptions is formulated as a decentralized partially observable Markov decision process (Dec-POMDP) and solved using a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework.
